E2E tests added (E2E-1 through E2E-6): isolated temp-dir tests for agents-only, dotfiles-only, and dotfiles+agents scenarios.


### CONFIG: Exclude rules/language.md from tracking (private symlink) (2026-04-25, pending)
Background: language.md is a private-repo symlink installed by my-private-repo/install. After agents-split, the symlink target lands at c:/git/agents/rules/language.md. Without a gitignore entry it would appear as untracked in the public agents repo.
Changes: Added rules/language.md to .gitignore.


### BUGFIX: block-dotenv: extend to Edit/Write/MultiEdit tools (2026-04-26, pending)
Background: block-dotenv hook only covered Bash/Read/Grep/Glob tools, leaving Edit and Write tools able to overwrite dotenv files. Also, git commit message stripping regex did not handle 'git -C <path> commit' form, causing hook to self-block when commit messages mentioned dotenv.
Changes: Extended block-dotenv.js to handle Edit, Write, MultiEdit tools via isDotenvPath() on file_path. Fixed commit stripping regex to match 'git -C <path> commit'. Added Edit/Write deny rules for dotenv files in settings.json. Updated hook matcher. Fixed stale HOOK path in test file. Added test coverage.


### FEATURE: GitHub Copilot support: shared instructions, hooks, and prompt files (2026-04-26, (pending))

Background: agents repo was Claude Code-only. Goal was to extend CLAUDE.md, rules/, hooks, and skills to GitHub Copilot (VS Code) without duplicating the SSOT. Research found that Copilot natively reads CLAUDE.md and ~/.claude/rules/ via chat.useClaudeMdFile, shares the same hooks JSON protocol as Claude Code and reads ~/.claude/settings.json directly, and supports .prompt.md slash commands. Subagent delegation (Agent tool) has no Copilot equivalent.

Changes: Added copilot/prompts/ with 8 core skills ported to .prompt.md (commit-push, update-docs, review-code-security, review-plan-security, survey-code, write-tests, make-plan, deep-research). make-plan rewritten as self-review single-pass (draft -> critique -> revise). Added install/win/vscode-settings.ps1 and install/linux/vscode-settings.sh to merge 8 required VS Code settings keys non-destructively with .bak safety. install.ps1 and install.sh call the new subscripts. settings.json hook matchers extended with Copilot tool names (runInTerminal, runCommands, editFiles) via OR patterns. README updated; docs/architecture/copilot.md added.


### CONFIG: Deny agent writes to .private-info-allowlist (2026-04-26, )

Background: Prevent accidental allowlist expansion: .private-info-allowlist must only be edited by humans with clear intent, not by the AI agent.

Changes: Add deny rules for Edit and Write on .private-info-allowlist to settings.json permissions.


### BUGFIX: workflow-gate: junction-aware docs evidence detection (2026-04-26, pending)

Background: workflow-gate hasStagedDocChanges() assumed docs/ lives in the same git repository as the source code. In repos where docs/ is a junction pointing to an external git repo, docs/ is gitignored in the source repo, so git diff --cached never shows staged doc changes there — making the docs gate permanently impassable.

Changes: Added resolveExternalDocsRepo() to workflow-gate.js: runs git rev-parse --show-toplevel from within docs/, compares to source repo root, returns the external repo root if they differ (null otherwise). Refactored hasStagedDocChanges() to check the source repo first, then fall back to the external repo if a junction is detected. Added missing path require(). Updated update-docs.prompt.md Completion section to document staging in the junction target repo. Added L2-k/L2-l tests to main-workflow-state-machine.sh covering junction-present+staged->approve and junction-present+unstaged->block.


### CONFIG: Allow WebFetch for code.visualstudio.com; raise effortLevel to high (2026-04-26, pending)

Background: VS Code Copilot documentation (code.visualstudio.com/docs/copilot/) is frequently referenced. effortLevel raised from medium to high.

Changes: Added WebFetch(domain:code.visualstudio.com) to permissions.allow. Set effortLevel to "high".


### CONFIG: Add dotfiles/my-private-repo/agents to additionalDirectories (2026-04-26, pending)

Background: Cross-repo visibility: when working from any of dotfiles/my-private-repo/agents, the other two should be visible as additional directories. Confirmed via testing that additionalDirectories relative paths are CWD-relative.

Changes: Added ../dotfiles, ../my-private-repo, ../agents to additionalDirectories in settings.json alongside existing ../my-specs-repo.


### CONFIG: Allow WebFetch for github.com and langfuse.com (2026-04-26, pending)

Background: Added github.com and langfuse.com to WebFetch allowlist for langfuse project research.

Changes: Added WebFetch(domain:github.com) and WebFetch(domain:langfuse.com) to permissions.allow in settings.json


### FEATURE: Add Claude Code and Codex install to agents installer (2026-04-26, (pending))

Background: installer only checked for claude; did not install it or Codex. Goal was to consolidate both AI CLI tools into agents/install.ps1 and install.sh, loosely coupled from dotfiles.

Changes: Added install/win/claude-code.ps1 (@anthropic-ai/claude-code via npm) and install/win/codex.ps1 (@openai/codex via npm). Added install/linux/claude-code.sh (native installer) and install/linux/codex.sh (npm/nvm). install.ps1: checks fnm — if missing, installs via winget then aborts with restart prompt; if present, runs both scripts then session-sync. install.sh: same pattern with nvm.


### FEATURE: 3-stage planning pipeline: clarify-intent / design-approach / make-detail-plan (2026-04-26, pending)
Background: The make-plan planner/reviewer loop worked well as an implementation-planning tool but had three structural gaps: (1) no background explanation surfaced to the user — the orchestrator suppressed all transcripts until APPROVED; (2) the planner/reviewer debate focused on file-level details before any high-level alignment, because the reviewer checklist had no direction-validity axis; (3) no requirement-clarification stage existed. External frameworks (grill-me by Matt Pocock, GitHub Spec Kit /specify->plan->tasks, Kiro IDE requirements/design/tasks, Aider architect/editor split) all separate the three altitudes: requirements, high-level approach, implementation detail.
Changes: Split the Plan step into three sequential skills inside the existing workflow plan atom (workflow-state.js VALID_STEPS unchanged). (1) /clarify-intent: main Claude interviews the user via AskUserQuestion in decision-tree style with a recommended answer per question (max 5 rounds); writes session-id-intent.md to ~/.claude/plans/. Mandatory; hard-fails in non-interactive contexts. (2) /design-approach: approach-designer subagent proposes 2-3 mutually-exclusive high-level approaches (file paths and steps forbidden), approach-reviewer checks direction only (APPROVED / MISSING_ALTERNATIVE verdicts, max 2 rounds); writes session-id-approach.md. (3) /make-detail-plan (renamed from make-plan): existing planner/reviewer loop, now reads intent.md and approach.md as inputs; planner Background section replaces Goal. New agents: approach-designer (opus, 2-3 approaches required, file paths prohibited, SINGLE_APPROACH_JUSTIFIED escape, NEEDS_RESEARCH escape), approach-reviewer (opus, rigid 2-verdict format). rules/plan-skip.md centralizes skip conditions. CLAUDE.md Workflow step 2 expanded to 2a/2b/2c. workflow-gate.js SKILL_MAP.plan updated to show 3-skill chain.



### CONFIG: change hooksPath write target to ~/.gitconfig (2026-04-26, d8b7ee7)
Background: The agents installer was writing core.hooksPath into config.local, but since config.local is a symlink to my-private-repo's config.local.linux, this resulted in an absolute path being written into a tracked file. Switching to --global caused the value to be written to ~/.config/git/config instead (dotfiles-managed, tracked) due to XDG precedence, which is also a problem. To avoid polluting either tracked file, ~/.gitconfig was explicitly specified instead.
Changes: Changed install/linux/dotfileslink.sh and install/win/dotfileslink.ps1 to use `git config --file $HOME/.gitconfig core.hooksPath`. `~/.gitconfig` is a per-user local config file not tracked by any repository, making it an appropriate location for hooksPath.


### REFACTOR: Rebrand to Nirecode (2026-04-26, (pending))

Background: The README h1 was the generic name '# agents', making the framework hard to identify. To establish an independent brand tied to the GitHub handle 'nirecom', /deep-research verified naming conventions across 10 prominent Claude Code framework repositories from primary sources.

Changes: Changed README h1 to 'Nirecode: The self-driving agent coding framework — secure by default, reliable by design'. Added a <sub> line with pronunciation hint and supported tools, plus two feature bullets (self-driving state machine + OWASP-scoped test/security). Repository name 'agents' is kept unchanged for URL compatibility.


### FEATURE: Add cross-provider adversarial review via Codex CLI at workflow step 5 (2026-04-26, pending)

Background: The step 5 security review previously relied solely on Claude (claude-sonnet subagent via /review-code-security), leaving model-specific blind spots unchecked. Adding a second-provider review with OpenAI Codex CLI gives adversarial cross-model coverage — no single model's biases dominate.

Changes: Added bin/review-code-codex (bash script): detects codex CLI, runs adversarial diff review, always emits ## Codex Review: PERFORMED|SKIPPED|FAILED status line via Bash tool (user-visible without relying on Claude's summary), appends JSONL audit log to ~/.claude/projects/codex-review/. Added skills/review-code-codex/SKILL.md as thin wrapper. Updated CLAUDE.md step 5 to include review-code-codex as a third parallel call alongside test suite and /review-code-security. Added symlink installation in dotfileslink.sh (Linux) and review-code-codex.cmd launcher in dotfileslink.ps1 (Windows). Added tests/feature-review-code-codex.sh with 16 test cases covering SKIPPED/PERFORMED/FAILED status labels, visibility invariant, JSONL logging, timeout handling, shell injection safety, and idempotency.


### CONFIG: install.sh: add ANSI color output (2026-04-27, pending)

Background: On macOS/Linux, install.sh used plain echo with no color formatting, while the Windows install.ps1 already used Write-Host -ForegroundColor. The visual gap made the macOS installer harder to follow at a glance.

Changes: Added TTY-detected ANSI color variables (C_CYAN, C_GREEN, C_YELLOW, C_BOLD, C_RESET) to install.sh. === headers print in cyan, --- section markers in bold, === Done === in green, error/warning messages in yellow. No escape codes emitted when stdout is not a terminal (pipe/log safe).


### BUGFIX: profile-snippet.sh: BASH_SOURCE[0] undefined in zsh causes AGENTS_CONFIG_DIR=HOME (2026-04-27, 1a0c112)

Background: In zsh, BASH_SOURCE[0] is undefined; dirname received an empty string, resolving to CWD (typically $HOME) via cd ., so AGENTS_CONFIG_DIR was set to $HOME. All hooks resolved to $HOME/hooks/*.js, node could not find the files, and every hook silently fail-opened (approved everything).

Changes: Added ZSH_VERSION branch to profile-snippet.sh: use ${(%):-%x} in zsh to obtain the script own path instead of BASH_SOURCE[0].


### BUGFIX: install.sh incompatible with macOS zsh and symlinked rc files (2026-04-27, 9be47fd)

Background: install.sh hardcoded ~/.bashrc as the rc file target, so profile-snippet sourcing was never written for zsh users on macOS. Additionally, sed -i '' failed on symlinked rc files (macOS sed refuses in-place edit on symlinks). A separate bug caused an unbound variable error by referencing _rc_file after unset. vscode-settings.sh used GNU-only realpath -m which does not exist on macOS.

Changes: Detect default shell via $SHELL to write profile sourcing to .zshrc / .bashrc / .profile. Replace sed -i '' with perl -i to support symlinked rc files. Save _rc_file before unset to avoid unbound variable in the final message. Drop realpath -m in vscode-settings.sh; fall back to plain realpath with error suppression.


### BUGFIX: install.sh/ps1: restart message shown on every run; printf errors on non-tty (2026-04-27, staged)

Background: The restart-your-shell message was printed unconditionally at the end of install.sh and install.ps1, even when profile sourcing was already present and no restart was needed. Additionally, printf format strings starting with --- triggered an invalid option error in bash when color variables were empty (non-tty stdout, e.g. when piped).

Changes: Added _need_restart/_needRestart flag; only print the restart message when sourcing is newly added. Fixed all printf format strings that start with --- to use printf -- to prevent bash from interpreting --- as an option flag.

### BUGFIX: Fix HIGH findings in review-code-codex from Codex self-review (2026-04-27, pending)

Background: Running review-code-codex on its own diff revealed three HIGH-severity issues: (1) --base without argument caused unbound-variable crash before emitting status line, violating output contract; (2) git diff failures were silently treated as empty diff (SKIPPED) instead of FAILED, creating false coverage; (3) prompt was passed as command-line argument instead of stdin, risking OS arg length limits on large diffs. Also: PATH-filtering approach in tests broke once codex was actually installed (fnm path not filtered).

Changes: bin/review-code-codex: check $# before accessing $2 for --base; capture git diff stderr and emit FAILED on non-zero exit; pass prompt via stdin ('-') instead of command-line arg. tests/feature-review-code-codex.sh: add cases for --base-missing-arg and git-diff-fail; replace PATH-filtering with MINIMAL_PATH=/usr/local/bin:/usr/bin:/bin to reliably hide codex without removing bash/git.


### CONFIG: installer: gray color for already-done messages on Linux/WSL (2026-04-28, pending)

Background: On Windows, already-handled items use Write-Host -ForegroundColor DarkGray. On Linux/WSL, these were plain white echo — making re-runs harder to scan.

Changes: install.sh: added C_GRAY to color block, exported all color vars for child script inheritance. dotfileslink.sh: Symlinks created/core.hooksPath/Generated in green, backups in yellow. claude-code.sh and codex.sh: already installed in gray, installed in green. session-sync-init.sh: already exists in gray, initialized in green, Remote set to unchanged. Profile sourcing messages in green. check-cross-platform.js error message updated to specify --short hash.



### FEATURE: Worktree safety: rules, skills, and deny hardening (2026-04-28, pending)
Background: INCIDENT #2 (worktree deletion silently destroyed a gitignored dotenv file, making Langfuse volume passwords unrecoverable) revealed no skill-based procedure existed for worktree entry/exit. This adds structured guardrails: a rule for when to use worktrees, two skills encapsulating the entry/exit procedure, URL-safe secret generation guidance, and a risky-operations decision path requiring recovery options before destructive proposals.
Changes: Added rules/worktree.md (skill-only entry/exit policy + fit criteria; skill notice moved to top). Added skills/worktree-start (worktree setup + gitignored file classification and copy). Added skills/worktree-end (inventory, backup manifest, Docker bind-mount detection, dry-run summary, and safe removal). Added skills/create-key (URL-safe password generation: hex / base64url / percent-encode; opaque secret guidance; handling and storage rules). Added rules/ops.md (Risky Operations Decision Path: enumerate recovery options before any destructive command). Added two deny entries to settings.json blocking Remove-Item -Recurse -Force (both arg orders).


### FEATURE: Codex-first plan review: integrate Codex as primary plan reviewer (2026-04-28, pending)
Background: review-code-codex at Step 5 showed good results. Manually feeding plan-mode output to Codex surfaced different perspectives from Claude reviewer. Goal: integrate Codex as the primary reviewer in make-detail-plan and design-approach loops to improve plan quality and load-balance Claude token costs.
Changes: Added bin/lib/codex-core.sh: shared library (codex_core_init, codex_core_adversarial_preamble, codex_core_check_cli, codex_core_run, codex_core_log, codex_core_emit_failed). Added bin/review-plan-codex: CLI with --input <file> --format {detail-plan|approach}; emits Codex Plan Review: PERFORMED/SKIPPED/FAILED; always exits 0. Refactored bin/review-code-codex to source codex-core.sh (public interface unchanged); added adversarial preamble to prompt. Updated skills/make-detail-plan and skills/design-approach to codex-first review loop each round; fall back to Claude reviewer subagent on SKIPPED/FAILED/malformed with explicit user-visible message. Added fallback-role notes to agents/reviewer.md and agents/approach-reviewer.md. Added review-plan-codex launchers to install/linux and install/win. Added tests/feature-review-plan-codex.sh (22 cases); added preamble assertion to feature-review-code-codex.sh. Fixed symlink source path bug in both bin scripts (dirname realpath).


### BUGFIX: workflow-gate: auto-detect repo via additionalDirectories (2026-04-28, pending)
Background: When git commit ran without -C from a dotfiles-primary session, resolveRepoDir used CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR (dotfiles), so evidence checks looked for staged tests/docs in the wrong repo.
Changes: resolveRepoDir now checks CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR for staged changes first; if none, scans additionalDirectories from settings.json via __dirname. Falls back when nothing is staged. Fixed stale path in fix-workflow-gate-unix-path.sh; added H1-H3 detection tests.


### REFACTOR: Rename design-approach to make-outline-plan (2026-04-28, pending)
Background: The design-approach skill name caused confusion with UI design work and lacked symmetry with make-detail-plan. Renaming to make-outline-plan (outline vs detail) makes the pairing clear.
Changes: Renamed skill design-approach to make-outline-plan (directory + SKILL.md), subagents approach-designer to outline-planner and approach-reviewer to outline-reviewer, completion marker WORKFLOW_DESIGN_APPROACH_COMPLETE to WORKFLOW_OUTLINE_PLAN_COMPLETE, and test feature-design-approach.sh to feature-make-outline-plan.sh. The --format approach CLI flag and <session-id>-approach.md output filename were left unchanged (internal artifact labels). Updated 17 files across agents and my-private-repo repos.


### FEATURE: Add next-step transition hints to SessionStart and workflow-mark (2026-04-28, pending)
Background: SessionStart and workflow-mark only emitted session_id in additionalContext, so when users gave instructions without naming a skill (e.g. 'start langchain phase 5'), Claude would skip clarify-intent and jump straight to implementation. The only enforcement point was the commit-time workflow-gate.js with no guidance during the session.
Changes: hooks/lib/workflow-state.js: added NEXT_STEP_HINT table (step to next-skill instruction string) and nextStepHint(step) function, both exported. hooks/session-start.js: added buildWorkflowStatus() that outputs all 9 step statuses and a NEXT ACTION line in additionalContext on every SessionStart. hooks/workflow-mark.js: after each successful markStep() call in all sentinel handlers, appends nextStepHint() result to additionalContext as a transition hint. 8 SKILL.md files (clarify-intent, make-outline-plan, make-detail-plan, survey-code, deep-research, write-tests, review-code-security, update-docs): Completion sections converted to numbered lists with explicit next-skill Skill tool invocation step after the sentinel echo. tests/feature-workflow-transition-hint.sh: 19 test cases (SS-1 to SS-4, WM-5 to WM-12).


### FEATURE: session-sync: sync plans/ across machines (2026-04-29, (pending))
Background: Plan files written to ~/.claude/plans/ (e.g. *-intent.md generated by clarify-intent, make-outline-plan, make-detail-plan) were not included in session-sync. When continuing work on another machine, previously created plan files were unavailable.
Changes: Added plans sync to push/pull/reset in bin/session-sync.sh and bin/session-sync.ps1. Push: copies $CLAUDE_DIR/plans/ into $PROJECTS_DIR/plans/ before git add. Pull/reset: copies files from $PROJECTS_DIR/plans/ into $CLAUDE_DIR/plans/ (merge — local-only files are preserved). Skips silently when plans/ is absent on either side. Added 10 assertions (8 test cases) to tests/main-session-sync.sh and 4 Pester tests to tests/main-session-sync.Tests.ps1. All new plans tests pass.


### REFACTOR: Subagent model/effort optimization + planner rename + outline.md unification (2026-04-29, pending)
Background: Token consumption was reaching 80-100% of Opus session budget in a single workflow run. Community research (wshobson/agents, VoltAgent, davepoon) confirmed the industry pattern: Opus for critical review only, Sonnet for standard work, effort:high reserved for heavy decision-making roles (doctor/triage equivalents). This refactor aligns agent configuration with that practice.
Changes: model: sonnet — clarify-intent, make-outline-plan (orch), make-detail-plan (orch), detail-planner (ex planner), write-tests. model: opus / effort removed — outline-planner, outline-reviewer. model: opus / effort:high kept — detail-reviewer (ex reviewer). model: opus / effort:high added — review-code-security (security audit is the heaviest decision). effort:low removed — survey-code, update-docs. Progressive disclosure added to outline-planner and detail-planner: start with docs/architecture.md + docs/todo.md, Grep before Read, max 8 source files, do not re-read rules/ (already in system prompt). Renamed planner.md -> detail-planner.md and reviewer.md -> detail-reviewer.md for explicit role distinction. Renamed session artifact approach.md -> outline.md throughout. Split clarify-intent output into intent.md (Background/Scope/Constraints only, passed downstream) and intent-log.md (Q&A transcript, not passed downstream) to reduce context copied into each subagent prompt.


### FEATURE: document plan mode incompatibility (2026-04-29, pending)
Background: Plan mode restricts Skill tool invocations, causing the mandatory workflow to be silently skipped. A SessionStart hook approach was investigated but hook additionalContext is not surfaced to Claude in plan mode, making it ineffective.
Changes: Added plan mode incompatibility notes to README.md (before Docs-only short-circuit bullet) and CLAUDE.md (new section before Docs-only Short-circuit).


### BUGFIX: Fix cleanup STEP_HINT sentinel format (2026-04-29, pending)
Background: STEP_HINT for cleanup used ':main' suffix in the sentinel, not matched by workflow-mark.js regex (WORKFLOW_MARK_STEP_cleanup_skipped: main).
Changes: Corrected to echo '<<WORKFLOW_MARK_STEP_cleanup_skipped>>' without suffix.


### FEATURE: Add show-diff.js: display-only PreToolUse hook for code diffs (2026-04-29, pending)
Background: require-diff-approval.js was a blocking PreToolUse hook requiring /tmp/diff-approved-<HASH> token files before each Edit/Write. It was never registered in settings.json so diff display never worked, causing frequent incidents where code changes were made without showing diffs in chat.
Changes: Replaced require-diff-approval.js with show-diff.js: a display-only PreToolUse hook that outputs systemMessage with diff preview (no decision field, no blocking). Registered in ~/.claude/settings.json PreToolUse with timeout 10.


### BUGFIX: fix: suppress create/delete mode output in session-sync on macOS (BSD grep compat) (2026-04-29)
Background: macOS (BSD grep) does not support \s in ERE mode (-E flag). The pull action filter '^\s*(create|delete) mode ' used grep -Ev which silently failed to match on macOS, letting all create/delete mode lines through. Additionally, git commit -q on macOS can still emit create/delete mode lines to stdout when new files are staged.
Changes: bin/session-sync.sh pull: replaced \s with [[:space:]] (POSIX character class, works on both BSD and GNU grep). bin/session-sync.sh push: added >/dev/null to initial commit to suppress stdout create/delete mode output on macOS git.


### BUGFIX: session-sync push failure on UD conflict + hooksPath always applied (2026-04-29, pending)
Background: session-sync push failed silently after 3 retries when rebase encountered a delete/modify conflict (file deleted by remote, still present locally). The case block only handled *.jsonl, leaving *.json workflow files unresolved → rebase --abort → push failure. Separately, session-sync-init only set core.hooksPath=/dev/null at first git init; if agents was reinstalled (writing ~/.gitconfig) without re-running session-sync-init, the projects repo inherited the agents hooks and blocked rebase --continue commits.
Changes: bin/session-sync.sh: added *) catch-all to conflict case (git rm to accept remote deletion); rebase --continue now falls back to rebase --abort on failure. install/linux/session-sync-init.sh and install/win/session-sync-init.ps1: moved core.hooksPath setting outside the init-only block so it is always applied on every run.


### CONFIG: settings: fix Write allow glob for intent-log.md (2026-04-29, pending)
Background: Added a Write allow rule to settings.json to suppress the permission dialog when clarify-intent writes intent-log.md. The initial pattern ~/.claude/plans/*-intent-log.md did not match because tilde is not expanded to the full absolute path at match time.
Changes: Changed allow rule to Write(**/.claude/plans/*-intent-log.md) so it matches the full absolute path.


### CONFIG: Move personal WebFetch domains to settings.local.json (2026-04-29, pending)
Background: Some WebFetch domains in the settings.json allow list were personal-workflow-specific or framework-specific and not appropriate to keep in a public repo.
Changes: Removed WebFetch(domain:ecc.tools), WebFetch(domain:langfuse.com), WebFetch(domain:docs.langchain.com), and WebFetch(domain:reference.langchain.com) from settings.json. These four entries are moved to settings.local.json (gitignored), whose source file lives in my-private-repo and is deployed via dotfileslink symlink.


### CONFIG: settings: cover both intent-log.md filename formats in allow pattern (2026-04-29, pending)
Background: Two filename formats exist for intent-log.md: UUID-based sessions produce <uuid>-intent-log.md, while the timestamp fallback produces intent-YYYYMMDD-HHMMSS-log.md. The previous fix (intent-*-log.md only) missed the UUID format.
Changes: Added both Write(**/.claude/plans/*-intent-log.md) and Write(**/.claude/plans/intent-*-log.md) to cover both filename formats.


### FEATURE: installer platform guards for install.sh/install.ps1 (2026-05-01, pending)
Background: install.sh had no guard against running in Git Bash/MSYS2/Cygwin on Windows. install.ps1 had no guard against running on Linux/macOS via PowerShell Core 7+.
Changes: install.sh: added uname -s check blocking MINGW*/MSYS*/CYGWIN* with printf %s format injection safety, placed before AGENTS_ROOT setup. install.ps1: added $IsWindows -eq $false guard before Set-StrictMode. Profile sourcing already-present message changed Green to DarkGray in both scripts.


### BUGFIX: installer idempotency: gray display for already-done operations (2026-05-01, pending)
Background: Second run of install.ps1 triggered unnecessary secret injection (the secrets CLI ran even when the injected output file already existed in my-private-repo), showed incorrect symlink backup warnings (path comparison used raw Target string without normalization), always set core.hooksPath green, always regenerated launcher files green, always showed Session sync initialized green, and always wrote VS Code settings even when unchanged.
Changes: dotfileslink.ps1: added Write-Launcher helper for idempotent launcher writes (DarkGray if unchanged); fixed symlink path comparison with GetFullPath() normalization; added current-value check for core.hooksPath (DarkGray if unchanged). session-sync-init.ps1: added _changed tracking, remote URL check before set-url, DarkGray already-up-to-date message when no-op. vscode-settings.ps1: compare each patch key before writing; skip write and backup when all keys already match. session-sync-init.sh: same idempotency fixes as PS version.


### BUGFIX: doc-append: initialize tail_text to suppress Pylance possibly-unbound warning (2026-05-01, pending)
Background: tail_text was assigned inside a for loop, so Pylance flagged it as possibly unbound at usage on line 152.
Changes: Added tail_text = "" initializer before the loop.


### FEATURE: Add repo-visibility tool for gh-based public/private detection (2026-05-01, pending)
Background: Needed a reliable way to check repo visibility (public/private) from Claude Code without per-repo CLAUDE.md maintenance. Direct gh command failed due to MSYS2 path mangling and lack of -C flag support in gh.
Changes: Added bin/repo-visibility.py: accepts Windows (c:/git/...) or WSL (/mnt/<drive>/...) path formats, normalizes per platform, runs gh repo view to output 'public' or 'private'. Added launchers in dotfileslink.sh (bash wrapper) and dotfileslink.ps1 (.cmd wrapper). Language policy updated in my-private-repo rules/language.md to use repo-visibility command.


### FEATURE: Assemble ~/.claude/settings.json from base + private extension (2026-05-02, pending)
Background: hooks (e.g. fornix-agent) require entries in settings.json but settings.json was a public-repo symlink, leaking private paths. settings.local.json does not support hooks. Solution: replace the symlink with an assembled real file. agents/settings.json stays public (base); agents/settings-extension.json (gitignored, symlinked from my-private-repo) holds private additions. assemble-settings.js performs schema-aware merge (hooks/permissions arrays concatenated; env/attribution object-merged; other keys extension-overrides base) and writes ~/.claude/settings.json on every install.
Changes: Add install/assemble-settings.js. dotfileslink.ps1/sh: remove settings.json symlink, add stale-symlink cleanup, call assembler. workflow-gate.js: read additionalDirectories from assembled ~/.claude/settings.json with fallback. .gitignore: add settings-extension.json. my-private-repo/install.{ps1,sh}: move agents installer to last (Step 6) so assembly runs after fornix hooks are injected into the extension file.


### FEATURE: Reset user_verification after git push; delete obsolete privacy.md (2026-05-02, pending)
Background: Once user_verification was marked complete it persisted for the rest of the session, allowing follow-up pushes without re-verification. This created a window where post-push bug fixes could be committed and pushed without user sign-off. rules/privacy.md contained obsolete .context-private/ instructions superseded by rules/private-info.md.
Changes: hooks/workflow-mark.js: hoist toolResponse/exitCode/sessionId; add push-detection block that resets user_verification to pending on successful git push; markStep failure emits FAILED message instead of silently succeeding. rules/privacy.md: deleted (obsolete).


### BUGFIX: profile-snippet.ps1: remove settings.json from symlink watchlist (2026-05-02, pending)
Background: profile-snippet.ps1 listed settings.json alongside CLAUDE.md as a symlink to monitor for breakage. However, settings.json is now an assembled file (written by assemble-settings.js), never a symlink. This caused the broken-symlink check to always fire, triggering dotfileslink.ps1 on every shell start even when nothing was broken.
Changes: Removed settings.json from the _agentSymlinks watchlist in profile-snippet.ps1. Only CLAUDE.md is now monitored.


### CONFIG: codex-core: increase review timeout 60s to 180s (2026-05-02, pending)
Background: Large plans (187+ lines) were timing out at 60s in review-plan-codex and review-code-codex. Diagnosed via ~/.claude/projects/codex-review logs — a 187-line plan hit the wall exactly at 60s.
Changes: bin/lib/codex-core.sh: changed timeout 60 to timeout 180; updated FAILED message to 'timeout (180s)'.


### BUGFIX: workflow-gate: expand env vars in resolveRepoDir (2026-05-03, pending)
Background: PreToolUse hook receives the command string before Bash expansion, so parseGitCArg returned the literal $FORNIX_DIR string. resolveRepoDir treated it as a path, causing hasStagedDocChanges to check the wrong directory and incorrectly block commits with git -C "$FORNIX_DIR" commit. Discovered during fornix-stream salvage bulk import (2026-05-02).
Changes: Expanded env vars ($VAR / ${VAR}) in resolveRepoDir via process.env lookup after parseGitCArg returns. Undefined vars resolve to empty string (falsy), falling through to the staged-changes fallback. Added test cases N5/N6/N7 to fix-workflow-gate-unix-path.sh.


### FEATURE: AWS infrastructure access Phase 1a: scan skills + settings.json guards (2026-05-03, pending)
Background: Phase 1a of AWS account access infrastructure. Goal: auto-collect full account configuration into AWS_STATE_DIR without opening the console. Claude Code gets AWS operation guards via settings.json and ready-to-use scan skill stubs.
Changes: settings.json: added 18 deny patterns (delete/terminate/remove/detach/release/destroy/etc.) and 14 ask patterns (create/put/update/start/stop/etc.) for aws CLI operations. New SKILL.md stubs: aws-scan (orchestrator), aws-scan-resources (EC2/S3/RDS/VPC/IAM), aws-scan-security (IAM posture/public exposure/security services), aws-scan-cost (Cost Explorer top-5), aws-scan-apps (ECS/Lambda/ALB/API GW topology). docs/architecture/claude-code/settings.md: AWS IAM Posture section added. docs/todo.md: IAM follow-up tasks added.


### FEATURE: judge-task-complexity + boost: complexity routing for subagent model selection (2026-05-03, pending)
Background: Sonnet weekly token consumption was outpacing Opus. VSCode + Claude Code cannot switch models mid-thread, so the solution uses the Agent tool model: override at subagent launch time. Two mechanisms: manual per-task Opus delegation (/boost) and automatic complexity-based routing (judge-task-complexity integrated into make-detail-plan and write-tests).
Changes: Added skills/judge-task-complexity/SKILL.md: dedicated complexity evaluator returning VERDICT: opus|sonnet | <signal IDs>. Six signals: S1-multi-file, S2-architecture, S3-security, S4-installer, S5-breaking, S6-long-plan. 1+ signal triggers opus; parse failure defaults to opus. Added skills/boost/SKILL.md: per-task Opus delegation via Agent tool (subagent_type: general-purpose, model: opus, mode: auto); empty-args guard included. Modified skills/make-detail-plan/SKILL.md: inserted step 2 (judge call + Model selected: text output), renumbered downstream steps, fixed missing step 5 gap; Skip Conditions updated to say skip judge-task-complexity explicitly. Modified skills/write-tests/SKILL.md: inserted step 5 (judge call + model display), renumbered old 5->6 and 6->7; subagent launch now passes model: param. Added tests/main-task-complexity-routing.sh: 39 structural grep tests, all passing.

Background: Session-sync auto-fetch and the codes function lived in dotfiles' install/win/profile.ps1 and .profile_common, so machines without dotfiles installed had no automatic sync.

Moved tests/main-wait-vscode-window.* and tests/main-profile-codes.* and updated path references to agents repo. Updated docs/architecture/claude-code/session-sync.md.


### FEATURE: CHANGELOG.md workflow: doc-append --commits optional + update-docs integration (2026-05-04, 2ac70af)
Background: No user-facing changelog existed for the public agents repo. /update-docs had no mechanism to write one simultaneously with history.md.
Changes: doc-append: --commits made optional (omitted produces date-only header). update-docs SKILL.md: Completion now calls doc-append twice — docs/history.md (internal, with --commits) and CHANGELOG.md (user-facing, without --commits). docs-convention.md: CHANGELOG.md added to Standard Files and Append-Only Tools. tests/feature-doc-append-wrapper.sh: 2 test cases for --commits omitted.

Background: Wanted Mermaid diagram generation (for README workflow chart) and Gemini CLI as a third AI provider. Codex/Gemini were previously installed unconditionally; made optional behind --develop flag to lower the default install bar.

Added bin/draw-diagram (mmdc SVG renderer) and bin/draw-diagram-gemini (Gemini API image generation). Added install/linux/gemini.sh and install/win/gemini.ps1 to install Gemini CLI and mmdc via npm. Added draw-diagram + draw-diagram-gemini launchers to dotfileslink.{sh,ps1}. install.ps1/-Develop and install.sh/--develop now gate both Codex and Gemini+mmdc install (default: Claude Code only). my-private-repo install.{ps1,sh} pass flags through to agents installer. Added workflow Mermaid flowchart to README.md. Updated README.md install commands to document -Develop option. doc-append --commits made optional for CHANGELOG.md use case.


### SECURITY: Block CC writes to .private-info-allowlist via PreToolUse hook (2026-05-04, 27afc41)
Background: permissions.deny for Edit/Write is bypassed in VSCode ask-before-edits mode, allowing CC to freely append to the scan-outbound allowlist and effectively whitelist private patterns without user approval.
Changes: Added isAllowlistPath check to block-dotenv.js PreToolUse hook to block Edit/Write/MultiEdit on .private-info-allowlist. Removed dead permissions.deny rules for the same file from settings.json. Added write-protection note to docs/scan-outbound.md Allowlist section.


### REFACTOR: Symlink .private-info-allowlist to my-private-repo; keep .example as public template (2026-05-04)
Background: Adding host/project-specific allowlist entries (e.g. CHAT_API_KEY_*) directly to agents/.private-info-allowlist would leak private info names through the public repo. Mirror the existing blocklist pattern.
Changes: Removed agents/.private-info-allowlist from tracking (now gitignored). Added .private-info-allowlist.example as the public template. Added rules/claude-config-source.md note: docker compose --force-recreate is required when only environment values change.


### REFACTOR: Rename private-info-blocklist.example to .private-info-blocklist.example for dotfile naming consistency (2026-05-04)
Background: After introducing .private-info-allowlist.example, the existing private-info-blocklist.example (without leading dot) became inconsistent. Convention research confirms .env.example pattern (leading dot preserved) is the de facto standard.
Changes: Renamed private-info-blocklist.example to .private-info-blocklist.example. Updated README.md, docs/scan-outbound.md, and the allowlist example glob path.


### FEATURE: PreToolUse early gate enforcing clarify_intent before Edit/Write (2026-05-04)
Background: CLAUDE.md instructions and the SessionStart additionalContext NEXT-ACTION hint were both advisory and routinely ignored — Claude would dive straight into investigation/edits without running /clarify-intent. CC ecosystem research confirmed only PreToolUse + exit-code-2 hard blocking achieves reliable enforcement. The TodoWrite-first mandate had also been over-firing for trivial tasks.
Changes: Extended hooks/workflow-gate.js with an earlyGate dispatch that fires before the existing git-commit gate. While clarify_intent is pending, blocks Edit/Write/MultiEdit/editFiles/NotebookEdit unless the Write target resolves under ~/.claude/plans/ (skill output allowlist with path.resolve hardening). Read/Grep/Glob/Bash are intentionally NOT in the matcher so the clarify-intent skill itself can do codebase exploration without deadlocking. settings.json matcher widened: Bash|Edit|Write|MultiEdit|editFiles|NotebookEdit|runInTerminal|runCommands. CLAUDE.md line 5 TodoWrite mandate replaced with clarify-intent-first directive that defers checklist creation to the skill. clarify-intent SKILL.md Completion section now creates the 9-step TodoWrite checklist after emitting CLARIFY_INTENT_COMPLETE. Added tests/feature-clarify-intent-gate.sh: 13 tests covering pending-block, complete-approve, fail-open, plans-path allowlist, regression for commit gate, idempotency.


### FEATURE: scan-outbound: add warn: prefix for soft-block patterns (2026-05-04)
Background: Hard-only blocklist forced a binary trade-off between noisy false positives and weakened patterns. Suspicious-but-uncertain combinations (e.g. two common keywords colocated) had no middle option.
Changes: Add warn: prefix in .private-info-blocklist for soft-block patterns. Scanner exit codes extended to 0/1/2/3 (warn-only=2, usage error moved 2 to 3 — non-backward-compatible). PreToolUse JS hook returns block + reason 'Ask the user' on warn (no TTY use). Bash git hooks prompt y/N via /dev/tty when interactive, auto-block when not. Empty warn: lines are skipped with stderr warning. Includes 4 new tests and security review (no CRITICAL/HIGH).


### FEATURE: Multi-session safety — AGENT_AUTO_BRANCH enforcement (replaces heartbeat sibling detection) (2026-05-05)
Background: Multiple concurrent CC sessions on the same repo can race when committing to the default branch. The session began with a heartbeat-based detection design (per-repo .git/agent-state/<sid>.json with mtime TTL, scanned by pre-commit/pre-push). Implementation completed and tested before the user identified that heartbeats were unsuitable for multi-root VSCode workspaces — CC's cwd is the workspace's first folder, so heartbeats land in repo X while real commits go to repo Y, leaving the guard blind. The 'twisted relationship' was deemed accident-prone. Three alternatives explored: touch-based heartbeats (PostToolUse refreshes per-repo with a manifest), centralized heartbeats keyed by repo-hash, and reusing CC's transcript files as natural heartbeats. All observe a proxy rather than enforce the actual invariant. The user proposed a fourth path: eliminate the race by construction — agents never write to the default branch by default. Codex independently endorsed this strongly: comparable to GitHub's protected branches, but enforced at edit-time rather than push-time. Hard pivot. Detailed design exploration in my-specs-repo research-results/multi-session-design-pivot.md.
Changes: Removed heartbeat infrastructure: hooks/lib/heartbeat.js, hooks/lib/check-sibling-heartbeat.sh, hooks/session-end.js, hooks/pre-push, hooks/user-prompt-submit.js, tests/feature-multi-session-coordination.sh; reverted heartbeat blocks in session-start.js, workflow-mark.js, pre-commit. Cleaned legacy agent-state heartbeat directories. Added AUTO_BRANCH enforcement: hooks/auto-branch-guard.js (PreToolUse Edit|Write|MultiEdit blocks default-branch edits), bash defense in pre-commit (commit-time mirror). Default-branch detection chain: origin/HEAD then local main/master then init.defaultBranch then fallback main; AGENT_DEFAULT_BRANCHES env override. Truthy on|1|true|yes|enabled (default ON); falsy off|0|false|no|disabled. Allows unborn HEAD, detached HEAD, non-git paths. Added push-boundary detection: workflow-mark.js records last_pushed_sha into workflow state on push; hooks/post-push-workflow-reset.js (UserPromptSubmit) resets branching_decision to pending when HEAD matches last_pushed_sha. Added helpers: hooks/lib/load-env.js parses AGENTS_CONFIG_DIR per-user environment file into process.env (existing env wins); hooks/lib/path-normalize.js exposes normalizeCwd (Windows POSIX drive-letter conversion) and resolveRepoCwd (5-level priority: dash-C path then CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR then input.cwd then state.cwd then process.cwd()). Updated CLAUDE.md Step 3 (Branch/Worktree decision to creation) with explicit AGENT_AUTO_BRANCH on/off case-split; rules/branch.md and rules/worktree.md updated. Tests: tests/feature-auto-branch.sh 37 cases. Bug-fix sequence (R1, F1-F4) found by external verifier session: R1 environment file not loaded; F1 UserPromptSubmit envelope contract mismatch (needs hookSpecificOutput); F2 workflow-mark missing cwd option (read wrong repo HEAD); F3 Windows POSIX-style path rejected by execSync; F4 PostToolUse stdin lacks cwd, fell back to wrong process.cwd(). Each fix added a regression test reproducing the production failure.


### REFACTOR: clarify-intent: drop summary-confirmation AskUserQuestion (2026-05-05)
Background: User feedback: the skill asked twice — once at intent.md Write (reviewable diff), then again via AskUserQuestion on the summary. The second prompt added friction without new information; users can interject freely on the summary text.
Changes: Removed step 5 (AskUserQuestion summary gate) from skills/clarify-intent/SKILL.md. Step 4 now notes that no confirmation gate is needed and that later planning stages provide revision opportunities.

### BUGFIX: workflow-gate: fix readStdin shared-buffer corruption causing large-payload fail-safe blocks (2026-05-06)
Background: workflow-gate.js readStdin used Buffer.alloc(4096) with buf.slice() in a loop. buf.slice() returns a view into the shared buffer, so subsequent loop iterations overwrote previous chunks, corrupting the accumulated input. Large Write/Edit/Bash heredoc payloads exceeded 4096 bytes and triggered JSON.parse failure, causing fail-safe block on every large content operation. User applied a temporary workaround in main; fix now landed in worktree.
Changes: hooks/workflow-gate.js readStdin: replaced chunk-accumulation loop with fs.readFileSync(0, 'utf8') — reads all stdin atomically, eliminating buffer aliasing and chunk-boundary issues.


### FEATURE: Parallel sessions worktree workflow: enforce-worktree, path layout, mandatory PR (2026-05-06)
Background: Three failure modes in multi-session parallel work: (1) main-branch leak — agents writing directly to the main checkout on any branch; (2) inconsistent worktree path schemes across sessions; (3) no mandatory PR + cleanup discipline after merge. auto-branch-guard only blocked protected-branch commits, not main-checkout writes from non-protected branches.
Changes: Renamed auto-branch-guard.js -> hooks/enforce-worktree.js; extended to detect main checkout via --git-common-dir==--git-dir (blocks all writes from main checkout regardless of branch). Added hooks/lib/bash-write-patterns.js: 46-pattern write classifier (POSIX redirects, here-docs, PowerShell cmdlets/aliases/encoded, file ops including npm/pip/cargo/go install, git/gh mutating subcommands). Added hooks/lib/worktree-config.js: WORKTREE_BASE_DIR env, validateTaskName/validateRepoName (injection-safe via [a-zA-Z0-9_-]+ allowlist), buildWorktreePath. Added install/linux/global-gitignore.sh and install/win/global-gitignore.ps1: idempotent XDG gitignore block manager for WORKTREE_NOTES.md; integrated into install.sh and install.ps1. Renamed env vars: AGENT_AUTO_BRANCH -> ENFORCE_WORKTREE, AGENT_DEFAULT_BRANCHES -> DEFAULT_BRANCHES; added AUTO_MERGE_PR, WORKTREE_BASE_DIR; migration fallback with deprecation warnings wrapped in BEGIN/END temporary markers. Rewrote skills worktree-start/worktree-end/commit-push: standardized two-level path layout <base>/<task>/<repo>, mandatory PR flow with AskUserQuestion merge prompt, gitignored state inventory before worktree removal. Added docs/parallel-sessions.md. Updated settings.json hook matcher Bash|Edit|Write|MultiEdit. Updated rules/branch.md (ENFORCE_WORKTREE note), rules/worktree.md (removed When-to-Use table — now controlled by env var). 5 TDD test files, 211 cases.


### BUGFIX: global-gitignore.ps1: .Count error on empty Where-Object result (2026-05-06, TBD)
Background: install.ps1 errored at line 50 of global-gitignore.ps1 with 'The property Count cannot be found on this object' on first run. PowerShell unwraps single/null pipeline results, so Where-Object returning 0 items produced $null; .Count then failed. Test coverage was bash-only (tests/feature-parallel-sessions-worktree-installer-ignore.sh tests global-gitignore.sh); the PS sibling had zero coverage.
Changes: Wrap Where-Object pipelines in @() at lines 50-51 so the result is always an array. PS-side test coverage tracked in todo.md.


### FEATURE: commit-gate redesign: merge boundary enforcement + staged diff fallback (2026-05-07, pending)
Background: Three design issues in the commit gate: (1) user_verification gate fired on feature-branch commits inside linked worktrees. (2) workflow-mark.js reset user_verification after any git push including feature-branch pushes. (3) review-code-codex could not use staged diff when no commits existed past base.
Changes: hooks/lib/merge-detect.js new module classifies gh-pr-merge / git-push-protected commands. hooks/lib/parse-git-args.js added parseGitGlobalOptions. hooks/workflow-gate.js added merge gate and worktree commit skip. hooks/workflow-mark.js narrowed push reset to protected-branch push or gh pr merge. bin/review-code-codex added staged diff fallback. skills/commit-push/SKILL.md removed AUTO_MERGE_PR env check. Tests: 24 cases in feature-commit-gate-redesign.sh, 26 in main-merge-detect.sh.


### BUGFIX: enforce-worktree: allow worktree lifecycle commands from main checkout (2026-05-07)
Background: git worktree add/remove/prune and the PowerShell directory-creation cmdlet are meta-operations that by design must run from the main checkout — they write .git/worktrees/ metadata and create the worktree directory. enforce-worktree.js was classifying these as write operations and blocking them, creating a circular bootstrap dependency: the fix requires creating a worktree, but that is exactly what was blocked.
Changes: Added isAllowedWorktreeCommand() and isAllowedNewItemDirectory() helpers in enforce-worktree.js. Both functions reject shell-chained commands (pipe, semicolon, ampersand in unquoted segments) and reject target paths that resolve inside the main repo root. The directory-creation helper is fail-closed when no path argument can be resolved. The -ItemType flag detection avoids a leading word-boundary anchor because the flag starts with a dash (non-word char). Added helpers stripQuotedSegments() and hasShellChaining() for safe chain detection across single and double-quoted segments. New test file tests/refactor-branching-complete-rename-worktree.sh: 19 cases (WL-1..12 for git worktree variants, NI-1..7 for directory creation variants).


### REFACTOR: rename workflow step branching_decision → branching_complete and sentinel WORKFLOW_BRANCHING_DECIDED → WORKFLOW_BRANCHING_COMPLETE (2026-05-07)
Background: The step name "branching_decision" implied a deliberate choice, but with ENFORCE_WORKTREE=on a worktree is mandatory — no decision is made. "branching_complete" aligns with the _COMPLETE naming used by all other sentinels (CLARIFY_INTENT_COMPLETE, WORKFLOW_OUTLINE_PLAN_COMPLETE, etc.) and with the new sentinel name. The CLAUDE.md label had already been updated to "Branch/Worktree creation" in a prior commit.
Changes: Renamed step key across hooks/lib/workflow-state.js (VALID_STEPS, STEP_HINT), hooks/workflow-mark.js (new BRANCHING_COMPLETE_RE_DQ/LOOKSLIKE_RE regexes, handler updated), hooks/workflow-gate.js (SKILL_MAP entry), hooks/post-push-workflow-reset.js (markStep target and message text), hooks/stop-cleanup-reminder.js (state key reference). Updated CLAUDE.md record line and skills/make-detail-plan/SKILL.md Completion section. Backward compat: old sentinel WORKFLOW_BRANCHING_DECIDED still accepted (both DECIDED and COMPLETE regexes are OR-chained in isSentinel() and the match handler); readState() migrates old state JSON with branching_decision key to branching_complete on read. Updated architecture docs (workflow.md, settings.md). Updated all test state JSON fixtures; fixed AGENTS_DIR hardcoding in tests/feature-auto-branch.sh to use script-relative path so worktree tests use worktree hooks.


### BUGFIX: bash-write-patterns: here-doc regex false-positive on workflow sentinel echo (2026-05-07)
Background: The here-doc pattern had no position anchor, so echo "<<WORKFLOW_...>>" was classified as a write command and blocked by enforce-worktree.js from the main checkout. Real heredocs always follow a command with preceding whitespace; sentinel strings appear inside quoted arguments with a quote character immediately before <<, which the old pattern did not distinguish.
Changes: Added position anchor matching the posix-redirect pattern's anchor style. Quotation marks no longer satisfy the anchor, so quoted strings containing << are no longer misclassified. Added tests/fix-heredoc-false-positive.sh: 20 cases (FP-1..5 sentinel echo variants, HD-1..5 heredoc regression, EDGE-1..4 compound commands, IDEM-1..2, SEC-1..3). Existing 101-case suite also verified clean.


### BUGFIX: enforce-worktree: gh command whitelist refactor (Group A/B + session-scope) (2026-05-07, pending)

Background: enforce-worktree.js was falsely blocking coordination commands (gh pr create/edit/close/comment/review, gh issue create/edit/close/comment, gh repo create/edit/rename/archive) in VS Code multi-root workspaces. Root cause: bash-write-patterns.js catch-all gh patterns classified both safe metadata-only commands and destructive ones as write, subjecting all gh commands to worktree enforcement. Additionally, findRepoRootForBash() fell back to AGENTS_CONFIG_DIR (agents main checkout) instead of process.cwd(), causing cross-repo operations to resolve to the wrong repo.

Changes: Split gh WRITE_PATTERNS into Group A (coordination/metadata only, classified "read" — guard never fires) and Group B (content-modifying or destructive, classified "write" with session-scope check). Group A: gh pr create/edit/close/comment/review, gh issue create/edit/close/comment, gh repo create/edit/rename/archive. Group B: gh pr merge, gh issue delete, gh repo delete, gh release create/edit/delete/upload, gh api with POST/PUT/PATCH/DELETE in any flag form (-X, -XVERB, -X=VERB, --method, --method=). Added session-scope whitelist: Group B commands verify target repo is in CWD repo + ENFORCE_WORKTREE_EXTRA_REPOS (comma-separated env var, new in .env.example). Changed findRepoRootForBash() fallback from AGENTS_CONFIG_DIR to process.cwd(). Added 72 integration tests in tests/fix-enforce-worktree-gh-whitelist.sh; updated feature-parallel-sessions-worktree-bash-patterns.sh and feature-parallel-sessions-worktree-guard.sh.


### FEATURE: enforce-worktree: EXTRA_REPOS directory-scan fallback (depth 1) (2026-05-07)
Background: Users with many repos under a common root (e.g. C:\git) had to enumerate every repo path individually in ENFORCE_WORKTREE_EXTRA_REPOS. No mechanism existed to specify a parent directory and have its children discovered automatically.
Changes: getSessionRepoRoots() now falls back to a depth-1 directory scan when an EXTRA_REPOS entry is not itself a git repo root: fs.readdirSync finds immediate subdirs and resolveRepoRoot() tests each one. Mixed individual+parent entries supported; spaces around commas trimmed. gh write commands (Group B) now call done() after the session-scope check passes instead of falling through to the main/worktree check — gh operates on GitHub, not local files, so the worktree-locality restriction is inapplicable. Added tests/fix-extra-repos-dir-scan.sh: 22 cases (N1-N5 dir-scan normal, E1-E4 error/edge, IDEM1 idempotency, SEC1 metachar injection, INT1-INT2 integration).


### BUGFIX: isPathOutsideRepo: normalize POSIX drive-letter paths before path.resolve (2026-05-07)
Background: On Windows + Git Bash, git rev-parse --show-toplevel returns POSIX-style paths like /c/git/agents. When these were passed directly to path.resolve() in Node.js on Windows, the drive letter was misread as a directory component (e.g. /c/git/agents -> C:\c\git\agents), causing isPathOutsideRepo to return the wrong result and falsely blocking git worktree add for targets that are actually outside the repo.
Changes: Applied normalizeCwd() to both targetPath and repoRoot before path.resolve() in isPathOutsideRepo. normalizeCwd converts /c/foo -> C:\foo on Windows and is a no-op elsewhere; || fallback guards against undefined return on non-string input. Also fixed run_hook test helper to cd into MAIN_REPO so New-Item tests pass consistently when run from a linked worktree. Added to_unix_style_path helper and WL-13/WL-14 regression tests covering POSIX drive-letter external and in-repo path cases.


### BUGFIX: bash-write-patterns: /dev/null null-sink misclassified as write (2026-05-08)
Background: posix-redirect pattern (regex /(?:^|[\s;|&])(?:\d*)>>?(?!>|\d)/) classified any redirect token as write regardless of destination. Common read-only suppression patterns like `git status 2>/dev/null` and `ls >/dev/null` were blocked by enforce-worktree.js from the main checkout, blocking the workflow on every error-suppressing command.
Changes: Added negative lookahead `(?!\s*\/dev\/null(?=\s|[;|&]|$))` to posix-redirect regex. The `(?=\s|[;|&]|$)` terminator (not `\b`) ensures `/dev/null/foo` (a real file under a directory called null) remains a write — `\b` matched at l/ boundary and would have wrongly excluded subpaths. Coverage: 4 READ_CASES additions (`2>/dev/null`, `>/dev/null`, `&>/dev/null`, `grep ... 2>/dev/null`), new test_dev_null_compound function with 5 cases (compound + rm, compound + read, append form, /dev/null/foo subpath, `>/dev/null 2>&1` documented FP preserved). Windows NUL intentionally not excluded (POSIX pattern only; pwsh null-sink uses Out-Null).


### FEATURE: ENFORCE_WORKTREE_EXCLUDE bypass + doc-append-plain + EXTRA_REPOS separator migration (2026-05-08)
Background: The ENFORCE_WORKTREE gate blocked all commits from the main checkout, including low-risk append-only updates to files like todo.md that span multiple repos. Agents needing to record a quick backlog item in a shared todo had to create a per-task worktree just for the note. ENFORCE_WORKTREE_EXTRA_REPOS also used comma as its separator, inconsistent with the emerging semicolon convention.
Changes: Added ENFORCE_WORKTREE_EXCLUDE env var accepting semicolon-separated glob patterns (hooks/lib/glob-match.js: parseExcludePatterns, pathMatchesGlob, matchesAnyExcludePattern). When ALL staged files match at least one pattern the pre-commit main-checkout and protected-branch gates are skipped; the private-info scanner still runs. AGENTS_CONFIG_DIR fallback ensures the matcher works in normal hook installs without the env var set; matcher failures emit a diagnostic and fall back to enforce (fail-closed). Added bin/doc-append-plain.py: append-only plaintext write CLI for doc files. Migrated ENFORCE_WORKTREE_EXTRA_REPOS separator from comma to semicolon (enforce-worktree.js); .env.example updated with migration note. Added 3 test files: feature-enforce-worktree-exclude-glob.sh (38 cases), feature-enforce-worktree-exclude.sh (9 integration cases), test_feature-enforce-worktree-exclude-doc-append.py (14 pytest cases). Cross-platform launchers added to install/win/dotfileslink.ps1 and install/linux/dotfileslink.sh.


### BUGFIX: fix worktree-to-main flow: 6 hook bugs + security review hardening (2026-05-08)
Background: ENFORCE_WORKTREE introduction blocked the worktree to main merge workflow (PR create / merge / cleanup) via several PreToolUse hook bugs. Six known issues from todo.md plus security review findings from Codex and Claude reviewers were addressed in one PR.
Changes: bash-write-patterns.js: tightened git-branch-mutate regex to flag-position only (kills branch-name false-positives like agents-env-consolidate); reclassified git branch -d as read; added Group A heredoc/here-string override in classify so gh pr create --body has no false-positive; added git-update-ref to WRITE_PATTERNS. enforce-worktree.js: added isAllowedFastForwardMerge predicate so git pull/merge --ff-only is allowed from main checkout; wrapped Main block in require.main guard and exported the predicate for unit testing; extended hasShellChaining to detect command substitutions (necessary defense after security review). block-dotenv.js: replaced checkBashCommand with path-position parser (stripSubstitutions / tokenizeSegment / splitSegments / segmentBlocksDotenv); recurses into command substitutions before stripping (closes gh pr create --body $\(cat .env\) bypass); detects redirect targets before TEXT_CMDS short-circuit (closes echo > .env / printf >> .env bypass); recurses through bash -lc / sh -ic combined short flags; dropped -l/-a/-r short forms from TEXT_FLAGS to avoid Unix-flag collisions (closes wc -l .env / ls -l .env / cp -r .env bypass). Added 4 test files / 1 new file (fix-enforce-worktree-merge-gate.sh) covering all 6 changes plus security regression cases — 187 PASS / 0 new failures.


### BUGFIX: enforce-worktree Bundle A — EXCLUDE JS-side, session-scope Edit/Bash, git-commit regex (2026-05-09, c9f3c6a)
Background: Three daily-workflow blockers: (1) ENFORCE_WORKTREE_EXCLUDE was only honoured by pre-commit (shell), not by enforce-worktree.js PreToolUse — files matching EXCLUDE were blocked at the hook level even though pre-commit would have allowed them. (2) Edit/Write/MultiEdit and Bash branches lacked session-scope filtering — writes to non-session repos (e.g. ~/.claude/projects/.../memory/*.md) were blocked because findRepoRoot() and findRepoRootForBash() fell back to the session CWD repo. (3) git-commit regex matched any token containing 'commit', so git log -- hooks/pre-commit was classified as a write command.
Changes: bash-write-patterns.js: tightened git-commit regex to require 'commit' at subcommand position. New module bash-write-targets.js: extractRedirectTargets (POSIX > >>, skips FD-to-FD and /dev/null), extractTeeTargets, extractPwshWriteTargets (quote-aware tokenizePwsh, Move/Copy destination semantics), extractStagedFiles. enforce-worktree.js: added getExcludePatterns/isExcluded (gitignore basename semantics), collectBashWriteTargets, areAllBashTargetsOutsideSessionScope, isWriteTargetAllExcluded; hasCommandSequencing guard on compound commands (;/&&/||) to prevent session-scope allow bypass; Edit/Write/MultiEdit branch checks EXCLUDE then session-scope; Bash branch checks all extracted targets against session scope + EXCLUDE. Security: compound-command bypass (echo x > /tmp/out; rm README.md) blocked; PS quoted-path bypass blocked via tokenizePwsh. Tests: bash-write-patterns.sh +4 read-cases; new fix-enforce-worktree-bundle-a-targets.sh 37 unit cases; new fix-enforce-worktree-bundle-a.sh 31 integration cases.


### FEATURE: planner drafts persistence, intent+outline context merge, show-diff suppression (2026-05-10)
Background: Planner draft files written to OS temp (/tmp or %TEMP%) were lost to OS cleanup or context compaction, requiring planner subagent restart. review-plan-codex lacked intent.md context in the outline-plan branch and had no structured merge for detail-plan, causing out-of-scope reviewer concerns. show-diff.js previewed every draft revision to the user, interrupting the planner/reviewer loop. clarify-intent fallback session IDs used an intent- prefix that produced confusing filenames like intent-20260510-001819-intent.md.
Changes: make-outline-plan/make-detail-plan: drafts now written to ~/.claude/plans/drafts/; context file built from intent+outline sections and passed as --context. review-plan-codex outline-plan branch: added CONTEXT_BLOCK/CONTEXT_NOTE mirroring the detail-plan branch. show-diff.js: added isPlanDraftFile() to suppress previews for ~/.claude/plans/drafts/ only (final artifacts in ~/.claude/plans/*.md still show diffs). clarify-intent: fallback session ID changed from intent-YYYYMMDD-HHMMSS to YYYYMMDD-HHMMSS. docs/ops.md: new file with one-time migration runbook for renaming legacy intent-*.md files. Tests: +7 cases in feature-review-plan-codex.sh; new fix-planner-drafts-context.sh covering show-diff.js isPlanDraftFile suppression (4 cases).


### REFACTOR: marker-gated branch deletion + main worktree terminology cleanup (2026-05-10)
Background: PR #20 had reclassified `git branch -d/-D` as a read operation so the worktree-only-write hook would not block it, but this was location-axis thinking dressed up as taxonomy: the right axis is the target branch's worktree binding, not the current working tree. Combined with `Bash(*git branch -d/-D*)` deny rules in settings.json that intercepted the command before any hook could run, `/worktree-end` was unable to autonomously delete the local branch after squash-merge cleanup — the user had to drop to a terminal each time. Separately, the codebase was riddled with the project-internal jargon "main checkout" where Git's official term is "**main worktree**" (vs "linked worktree"), drifting away from upstream documentation.
Changes:
1. **Marker-file-gated branch deletion** (the autonomy fix). `bash-write-patterns.js` now classifies `git branch -d/-D` as write (alongside `-m/-M`/`-c/-C`). `enforce-worktree.js` exposes a single authorised path: a marker file at `<git-common-dir>/info/pending-branch-delete` containing the target branch name and the worktree path being removed. The hook (`isAllowedBranchDeleteViaMarker`) allows the command iff the marker matches the target AND the recorded path resolves under `WORKTREE_BASE_DIR`. Direct ad-hoc `git branch -d/-D` from any worktree (main or linked) is rejected — no settings.json deny needed. `/worktree-end` SKILL: step 6 reordered to write the marker before `git worktree remove`, switch from `-d` to `-D` (squash-merge produces a non-fully-merged local branch that `-d` refuses), and unconditionally remove the marker on both success and failure paths to prevent stale authorisation.
2. **Terminology cleanup**: replaced "main checkout" → "main worktree" across `enforce-worktree.js` (comments + reason strings), `pre-commit`, `workflow-gate.js`, `rules/branch.md`, `rules/worktree.md`, `skills/worktree-end/SKILL.md`, `CLAUDE.md`, `README.md`, `.env.example`, `docs/parallel-sessions.md`, `docs/architecture/claude-code/settings.md`, `docs/plan.md`, `docs/todo.md`, and 8 test files. `docs/history.md` and `CHANGELOG.md` historical entries left intact (records of past wording at the time).
3. **Settings.json deny removal**: dropped `Bash(*git branch -d*)` and `Bash(*git branch -D*)` from `permissions.deny` so the marker-gated path can actually execute.
Tests: new `fix-unblock-branch-d.sh` (26 cases) covering classify reclassification, marker match/mismatch, path-outside-base rejection, missing marker, chained-command rejection, CRLF acceptance, linked-worktree readability via shared `.git`, and end-to-end hook block-then-allow. Updated `feature-parallel-sessions-worktree-bash-patterns.sh` to assert `-d/-D` are write (replaces previous PR #20 read assertions). Pre-existing failures in `feature-parallel-sessions-worktree-guard.sh`, `fix-enforce-worktree-gh-whitelist.sh`, `refactor-branching-complete-rename-worktree.sh` (related to PR #18's session-scope semantics) are unchanged — verified identical to main.


### CONFIG: correct .env.example EXCLUDE comment about PreToolUse bypass (2026-05-10)
Background: The .env.example comment for ENFORCE_WORKTREE_EXCLUDE still claimed 'Edit/Write/MultiEdit/Bash tool gating is NOT bypassed', which has been false since PR #18 added EXCLUDE bypass to the PreToolUse hook for those tools. The stale comment caused at least one unnecessary worktree creation in a docs-only edit task.
Changes: .env.example: rewrote the comment to state that the PreToolUse hook ALSO bypasses Edit/Write/MultiEdit and Bash file-target writes when every target matches EXCLUDE, with bin/doc-append-plain remaining as the append-only convenience wrapper.


### CONFIG: rewrite .env.example EXCLUDE comment qualitatively (no PR refs) (2026-05-10)
Background: The .env.example entry added in the prior commit referenced PR #18 directly. Code-review feedback: .env.example is end-user config documentation and should describe what a setting does and doesn't do qualitatively, not reference internal change history. PR refs belong in history.md, not in user-facing config.
Changes: .env.example: replaced the prior 'PR #18 added X' phrasing with a structural breakdown listing what is bypassed (PreToolUse Edit/Write/MultiEdit + Bash file-target writes; pre-commit gate when all staged files match) and what is NOT bypassed (block-dotenv, scan-outbound, partial-staged commits). bin/doc-append-plain still called out as the convenience wrapper when EXCLUDE is unset.


### CONFIG: tighten .env.example to user-perspective + codify rule in docs-convention.md (2026-05-10)
Background: The qualitative rewrite of ENFORCE_WORKTREE_EXCLUDE in the prior commit still referenced internal hook names (block-dotenv, scan-outbound, PreToolUse) and tool-class breakdowns (Edit/Write/MultiEdit) that an end user reading .env does not know. Code-review feedback: .env.example should document only what the user can do, what they can't do, and the value format — strictly from a user-perspective.
Changes: Two changes: (1) .env.example: rewrote the ENFORCE_WORKTREE_EXCLUDE block to drop all internal hook/module names and tool-class jargon, replacing them with user-visible phrasing (e.g. 'secret protection' instead of 'block-dotenv'). Trimmed from ~17 lines to ~14 lines. (2) rules/docs-convention.md: added a new content rule for .env.example codifying the three required items (what you can do / what you can't do / format) and the explicit don't-include list (internal names, PR refs, implementation details), so future entries inherit the same lens.


### CONFIG: trim verbose 'do not include' subsection from .env.example convention (2026-05-10)
Background: The newly added .env.example content rule in docs-convention.md included a long 'Do NOT include in .env.example' subsection (4 bullets + a recap paragraph). Per code-review feedback, this was over-specified: the three required items already imply what is in scope, so the negative list was redundant noise.
Changes: rules/docs-convention.md: removed the 'Do NOT include' bullet list and the 'Use plain functional language' recap paragraph from the .env.example entry. The three positive requirements (what you can do / what you can't do / format) remain as the entire rule.


### CONFIG: retrofit CHANGELOG for PRs 19-22 + add CHANGELOG content rule + record write-pattern false-positive (2026-05-10)
Background: PRs 19-22 had updated docs/history.md but skipped CHANGELOG.md because the merging session called bare doc-append on history.md instead of routing through /update-docs (which writes both files for public repos). User flagged the gap. Same session also hit a write-pattern false-positive: POSIX file-copy and file-move tokens appeared in a doc-append subject argument, were classified as a write op, and blocked the command. This echoes the isBranchDeleteCommand subcommand-position regex issue logged earlier.
Changes: CHANGELOG.md: appended four user-facing entries summarising PR 19 (planner draft persistence + cross-stage context propagation), PR 20 (POSIX file-copy/move destination extraction + PowerShell migration runbook), PR 21 (branch deletion now requires /worktree-end marker + main worktree terminology alignment), PR 22 (ENFORCE_WORKTREE_EXCLUDE comment rewrite + .env.example content rule). PR 20 reverted git-branch read-classification deliberately omitted from CHANGELOG to avoid confusing readers upgrading past PR 21. rules/docs-convention.md: added a new content rule for CHANGELOG.md (write only user-felt changes: critical fixes, regressions, behaviour changes, configuration changes; do not include internal function or module or hook names). docs/todo.md: appended a new entry generalising the file-op-token substring false-positive cluster.


### FEATURE: global-gitignore.ps1: add Pester integration tests (2026-05-10)
Background: PS version of global-gitignore installer had zero test coverage; .Count null-ref bug first detected in production. Bash version was already covered by feature-parallel-sessions-worktree-installer-ignore.sh (15 cases).
Changes: Added tests/feature-parallel-sessions-worktree-installer-ignore.Tests.ps1 (Pester 5, 15 cases matching bash version): T01-T14 pass; T15 (unwritable parent dir via icacls) skips on TestDrive. Uses pwsh -File child process + TestDrive XDG isolation.


### FEATURE: worktree-start: .worktreeinclude gitignored-state automated copy (B-Hybrid Phase 1) (2026-05-10)
Background: worktree-start required manual file-copy for gitignored state (.env etc.), but enforce-worktree blocked the operation and Write tool blocked dotenv writes. Implemented B-Hybrid Phase 1: a Node.js CLI reads JSON from stdin and copies gitignored files matched by .worktreeinclude while enforcing .worktreecopyexclude denylist. Claude never reads file contents (blackbox copy guarantee).
Changes: Added bin/worktree-copy-include.js (stdin JSON -> copyInclude -> stdout JSON). Added hooks/lib/worktree-copy.js: enumerates gitignored files via git ls-files, applies ignore-package matchers for allowlist and denylist, path-traversal guard on source and destination, symlink rejection, mainRoot existence check. Added hooks/lib/worktree-include-match.js: thin ignore@5.3.2 wrapper. Added .worktreeinclude (default allowlist: .env/.env.local/.env.development/.env.test/.private-info-allowlist) and .worktreecopyexclude (denylist: .env.production/*.pem etc.). Updated skills/worktree-start/SKILL.md step 9 to use automated node pipeline. Added package.json with ignore@5.3.2. Security: destination bound check, symlink rejection, includeFile traversal guard, denylist negation stripping.


### FEATURE: user-escalation rule: autonomy-first, one-command-at-a-time, script-first (2026-05-11)
Background: CC had no rule governing when to ask the user to run a shell command. Two failure modes: (1) escalating to the user for operations CC could run itself via tools; (2) presenting multiple commands in batch, creating cascade risk if an intermediate command fails and the next one still runs against production state.
Changes: New rules/user-escalation.md with Decision Flow table (destructive check, CC-can-execute check, step count), Rule 1 Autonomy-First (attempt tools first, escalate only when genuinely blocked, no repeat asks), Rule 2 One-Command-at-a-Time (one command per ask, wait for result before next), Rule 3 Script-First (2 or more non-destructive steps bundled into a single fail-fast script). Precedence: ops.md beats Rules 1-3; Rule 1 beats Rules 2-3; Rule 2 beats Rule 3. New tests/feature-user-escalation.sh validates file structure, required headings, and Decision Flow ordering.


### FEATURE: GitHub Issues migration foundation: /issue-close, sub-issue gate, reconcile (2026-05-11, pending)
Background: todo.md files operated as flat open-issue lists with no ticket lifecycle. This PR adds the foundation layer for migrating per-repo work tracking to GitHub Issues: issue templates and label schema aligned with the history.md FEATURE/INCIDENT format, and a transaction-safe close pipeline ensuring every closed issue is atomically appended to docs/history.md before the ticket is shut.
Changes: rules/issues.md: ID-index format (- [ ] #N title), label schema (type:task/incident/priority:*), /issue-close as the sole authorised close path. .github/ISSUE_TEMPLATE/task.yml + incident.yml: issue forms with Background/Changes and Cause/Fix fields matching history.md entry format. .github/labels.yml + bin/sync-labels.sh: label definitions and idempotent gh-based sync. bin/issue-to-history.sh: fetches issue JSON via gh, parses all fields with a single node call (ASCII RS separator, no shell interpolation), calls doc-append; idempotent via ^### #N: anchor check. bin/issue-close-gate.sh: validates open sub-issues via gh api --paginate with multi-page awk sum; blocks parent close if any child is open. hooks/enforce-issue-close.js: PreToolUse hook blocking bare gh issue close commands not routed through the skill; bypass via ISSUE_CLOSE_SKILL=1 env var. skills/issue-close/SKILL.md: 9-step transaction-safe flow: state triage across 6 OPEN/CLOSED x sentinel combinations, pending->appended 2-phase sentinel commit on issue comments, sub-issue gate, doc-append, parent checkbox flip, gh issue close, todo.md cleanup. skills/issue-reconcile/SKILL.md: backfill skill scanning issues closed outside /issue-close (web UI, mobile, CLI). settings.json: enforce-issue-close.js PreToolUse hook entry added.


### FEATURE: Add CONFIRM_OUTLINE/DETAIL/WORKTREE/TESTS flags + get-config-var helper (2026-05-11, 5b530d0 56a2c7f)
Background: Confirmation prompts in make-outline-plan, make-detail-plan, worktree-start, and write-tests were unconditional, interrupting the workflow for users who wanted lower friction. Per-round planner/reviewer summaries also cluttered chat output unnecessarily.
Changes: New bin/get-config-var (POSIX bash) and bin/get-config-var.ps1 read AGENTS_CONFIG_DIR/.env via hooks/lib/load-env.js. The --is-off subcommand exits 0 when value is off/0/false/no/disabled (case-insensitive, no trim), mirroring enforce-worktree.js:69-72 semantics. Four CONFIRM_* flags (default on) added to .env.example: CONFIRM_OUTLINE, CONFIRM_DETAIL, CONFIRM_WORKTREE, CONFIRM_TESTS. Each gated skill checks the flag via bash -c get-config-var --is-off; when off the final result is shown and execution continues without AskUserQuestion. Per-round planner/reviewer chat noise suppressed: orchestrator emits only one status line per round; codex fallback messages go to session-id-{outline,detail}-debug.log instead of chat. Installer wiring: linux symlink and Windows .cmd/bash shim via dotfileslink. Tests: feature-confirm-flags-helper.sh (--is-off matrix, env precedence, path traversal, metachars, idempotency) and feature-confirm-flags-static.sh (skill wiring, installer presence, preserved instructions, removed legacy emit patterns).


### BUGFIX: strip-quoted-args: fix false positives in classify() and isBranchDeleteCommand (2026-05-11)
Background: isBranchDeleteCommand fired when a commit message contains branch -d (marker gate confused message text for a branch delete subcommand). classify() also fired on file-op terms (cp/mv/rm/touch) inside quoted shell arguments (e.g. doc-append subject values).
Changes: New hooks/lib/strip-quoted-args.js strips double-, single-, and ANSI-C-quoted content. classify() applies stripping only to file-op kind patterns via STRIP_KINDS. isBranchDeleteCommand() strips quoted args before branch-delete regex. Added interpreter-c write pattern to preserve write classification for bash -c and sh -c invocations.

Background: Session startup loaded all rules globally (~35,300 chars / ~8,800 tokens). test.md (107 lines), docs-convention.md (94 lines), coding.md (43 lines) loaded even when irrelevant. paths: frontmatter was silently ignored in user-level rules/ (Issue #21858); correct key is globs:.
