Group C: merged feedback_deep_research_source_quality and feedback_neutral_tone into feedback_third_party_references; cleaned feedback_force_push_policy; reindexed MEMORY.md. Tests: 31/31 PASS.


### FEATURE: delivery plan visible at outline stage; importance-first plan sections (2026-05-12)
Background: PR #26 (GitHub Issues migration) had a delivery plan variant buried at line 171 of detail.md without upstream agreement. The delivery plan (triage priority, execution order, split policy) is a higher-priority decision than implementation content, and leaving it implicit until detail.md was a structural gap in the planning workflow.
Changes: outline-planner.md: added required Delivery plan field to each approach Output Format; extended SINGLE_APPROACH_JUSTIFIED to two-line format with mandatory DELIVERY_PLAN: line. make-outline-plan/SKILL.md: added prose rationale preamble before AskUserQuestion; added DELIVERY_PLAN: parsing for single-approach path; updated Output Schema to include Delivery plan field. detail-planner.md: reordered plan sections to importance-first (Delivery plan -> Background -> Files to modify -> Steps -> Risks -> Out of scope). make-detail-plan/SKILL.md: inserted step 2 to surface outline delivery plan to main conversation before planner runs. clarify-intent/SKILL.md: added CONFIRM_OUTLINE flag check — when off, captures delivery plan direction one stage upstream. rules/docs-convention.md: appended Planning Pipeline Artifacts section. New test: tests/feature-delivery-plan-visibility.sh (10 static convention assertions).


### BUGFIX: settings.json push allow-rule + worktree-end marker auto-delete (2026-05-12)
Background: Two gaps blocked /worktree-end from completing cleanly: (1) git push -u origin <branch> from inside a linked worktree lacked a settings.json allow rule; (2) step 6g could not delete the pending-branch-delete marker because the deletion command is write-classified and blocked by enforce-worktree.js from main worktree CWD. Also make-outline-plan/make-detail-plan wrapped get-config-var in bash -c (also write-classified) and both had a defensive mkdir that the Write tool makes redundant.
Changes: settings.json: added Bash(git push -u origin *), Write(**/.git/info/pending-branch-delete), and two marker-delete allow rules. hooks/enforce-worktree.js: added isAllowedMarkerDelete predicate that allows deleting the exact marker path only when the recorded branch is gone (git show-ref exit 1); blocks on chaining, recursive flags, -Path wildcard, multi-target, branch still present, git fatal. Added extractRemoveItemPositionals helper. skills/worktree-end/SKILL.md: documented gate in step 6g. skills/make-outline-plan and make-detail-plan: removed bash -c wrapper from get-config-var and removed defensive mkdir. tests/fix-settings-marker-cleanup.sh: 23 cases covering predicate positive/negative, e2e hook, classifier stability, settings patterns.


### FEATURE: add planning skill allow rules: bash -c get-config-var, get-config-var, Read .claude/plans, printf debug log (2026-05-12)
Background: Planning workflow and write-tests/worktree-start skills invoked Bash commands and Read operations not in permissions.allow, causing permission prompts. Specifically: bash -c 'get-config-var' (used by clarify-intent/worktree-start/write-tests), get-config-var (used directly by make-outline-plan/make-detail-plan post-b7d6a97), Read /**/.claude/plans/** (skills read prior-stage artifacts), printf >> */.claude/plans/* (debug log for malformed codex output). review-plan-codex excluded as it has never prompted. mkdir rule omitted — Write tool auto-creates directories per clarify-intent/SKILL.md.
Changes: settings.json: added 4 allow rules (Bash(bash -c 'get-config-var *'), Bash(get-config-var *), Read(**/.claude/plans/**), Bash(printf * >> */.claude/plans/*)). New tests/feature-allow-rules-planning.sh: static test verifying presence, uniqueness, and placement of each rule. docs/todo.md: added follow-up items for npm install gap in worktree-start and .env/.private end-to-end copy verification.


### BUGFIX: worktree-end Step 6h: add git pull --ff-only after fetch (2026-05-12)
Background: After PR squash-merge, the local main branch was not fast-forwarded to origin/main, leaving it stale for subsequent work. The fix had to remain safe under concurrent sessions editing the same files.
Changes: skills/worktree-end/SKILL.md Step 6h now runs git -C <main> pull --ff-only immediately after git -C <main> fetch --prune origin. --ff-only halts on divergence rather than silently merging, which keeps concurrent-session setups safe. tests/fix-worktree-end-git-pull.sh adds four regression cases (literal string present, order after fetch, located within Step 6 section, no bare pull without --ff-only).


### BUGFIX: settings.json glob fix and worktree-CWD-safe get-config-var calls (2026-05-12)
Background: Two workflow-blocking bugs: (1) settings.json lines 86-87 used single * which cannot cross path separators, causing a permission prompt on every /worktree-end marker cleanup; (2) the 5 SKILL.md gates for CONFIRM_* flags called get-config-var from a linked worktree CWD where .env is absent. The helper fell back to defaults, so off-mode flags were silently ignored in worktrees.
Changes: settings.json: changed * to ** in two Bash allow rules for pending-branch-delete deletion. Added narrow allow rule for cd-prefixed get-config-var form (Bash(bash -c 'cd AGENTS_CONFIG_DIR && get-config-var *')). Wrapped each get-config-var --is-off invocation in 5 SKILL.md files (clarify-intent, make-outline-plan, make-detail-plan, worktree-start, write-tests) with bash -c 'cd AGENTS_CONFIG_DIR && ...' so load-env.js reliably locates .env regardless of worktree CWD. Also added a chaining/pipe caveat note to worktree-start SKILL.md step 5-6 documenting that enforce-worktree.js exemptions only apply to isolated commands.

### FEATURE: planning pipeline UX — narrow plans/drafts allow rules + show-diff Write preview (2026-05-12, pending)
Background: Two friction points: (1) settings.json had broad Write(**/.claude/plans/**) and Edit(**/.claude/plans/**) rules that do not match Windows backslash paths, causing VS Code permission dialogs for intermediate draft files written during the planner/reviewer loop. Final plan artifacts should still show dialogs — only drafts under plans/drafts/ should be silent. (2) show-diff.js Write branch displayed a plain header without a diff, and the editFiles tool was misrouted to the Write branch (content-less), producing empty previews.
Changes: settings.json: replaced two broad plans/** rules with six narrow drafts-only rules covering tilde, forward-slash, and Windows-backslash path forms. show-diff.js: Write branch reads existing file (1 MiB cap + isFile() symlink guard) and generates real diff -u for overwrite, or uses /dev/null as the old-side label for new files; makeDiff extended with opts.oldLabel/newLabel; tmpfile names use pid + 8-byte crypto random suffix; editFiles now correctly routes to the MultiEdit branch. Tests: feature-allow-rules-planning.sh extended with NARROW_RULES array, c2 placement check, d negative check; feature-show-diff-write-preview.sh added (7 cases).


### FEATURE: settings.json: permission update for --force-with-lease operations (2026-05-12)
Background: Deny rules blocked --force-with-lease by matching it as a substring of --force via glob, preventing auto-execution of rebase-cleanup on feature branches despite the policy permitting it.
Changes: settings.json: 3 deny patterns replaced with 6 anchored variants; 2 allow rules added for lease-based force flag. rules/git.md: Force section added. docs/todo.md: Policy section removed. docs/security-policy.md + architecture/settings.md: updated.


### BUGFIX: enforce-worktree: allow Write/Edit and no-op rm for pending-branch-delete marker (2026-05-12, 5de9026)
Background: The pending-branch-delete marker lives at <git-common-dir>/info/pending-branch-delete and is written by /worktree-end from the main worktree before authorising git branch -d. The enforce-worktree hook blocked Write/Edit to this path because it rejects all writes from the main worktree. Additionally, rm of a non-existent marker (stale cleanup after an aborted /worktree-end run) returned false fail-closed, causing an unnecessary permission prompt. The settings.json already had a Write allow rule for this path but it was ineffective because the hook fires before settings.json is consulted.
Changes: Added isMarkerFilePath helper to resolve the marker path via git rev-parse --git-common-dir and compare against the tool input path with cross-platform normalisation. Added Write/Edit exception in the main-checkout block gated by isMarkerFilePath. Fixed isAllowedMarkerDelete to return true on ENOENT (no-op delete is safe) and false on all other errors (fail-closed). Exported isMarkerFilePath. Updated tests: T5 renamed to marker_delete_allowed_no_marker (expected value flipped to true); added T22-T27 covering isMarkerFilePath matching and Write/Edit hook exceptions.


### BUGFIX: enforce-worktree: allow read-only CONFIRM_* probe from main worktree (2026-05-12, pending)
Background: Planning skills use bash -c to probe CONFIRM_* flags via get-config-var --is-off, but enforce-worktree.js classified all bash -c as writes and blocked the probe from the main worktree. CONFIRM_OUTLINE=off and CONFIRM_DETAIL=off were silently ignored in any session started from main.
Changes: Added isAllowedReadOnlyConfigCheck() to enforce-worktree.js: structurally validates three clauses (cd AGENTS_CONFIG_DIR, get-config-var --is-off KEY on|off, echo OFF with optional || echo ON), rejects backtick/dollar-paren/redirect/semicolon/bare-pipe variants. Call site added after isAllowedMarkerDelete. 15 test cases in tests/feature-parallel-sessions-worktree-guard.sh (4 positive, 11 negative). Coupling risk documented in docs/architecture/claude-code/workflow.md.


### FEATURE: workflow-gate: add --wip signal to skip user_verification for fixup commits (2026-05-12, pending)
Background: After the main implementation commit, small fixup commits triggered a full user_verification gate re-block, requiring repeated user approval for low-risk intermediate commits. The UX cost was disproportionate to the risk.
Changes: Added parseGitConfigValues() to hooks/lib/parse-git-args.js (quote-aware, pre-subcommand only). workflow-gate.js detects git -c workflow.wip=1 and skips only the user_verification step; run_tests, review_security, and docs gates still fire. State not mutated in the WIP path so user_verification stays pending and the next non-WIP commit re-blocks (idempotent). commit-push/SKILL.md: added ## WIP mode (--wip) section. WIP signal contract and coupling risk documented in docs/architecture/claude-code/workflow.md. 12 test cases in tests/feature-commit-gate-redesign.sh (Section F).


### FEATURE: Add test-runner subagent for Workflow Step 6 (2026-05-13, pending)
Background: Verbose test output ran in the main context, competing for token budget and obscuring planning context. Step 6 ran tests directly via Bash with a PostToolUse hook auto-marking run_tests, creating tight coupling between test output volume and context quality.
Changes: New agents/test-runner.md agent definition (tools: Bash/Read/Grep/Glob; model: sonnet): runs the test suite, returns a structured YAML summary (status/exit_code/duration/failing_tests/log_tail), never modifies files. CLAUDE.md Step 6 rewritten: three parallel calls (Agent test-runner + Agent security + Bash codex), then a sequential sentinel: run_tests_complete on pass, run_tests_pending on fail/timeout/runner-error. run_tests_pending overwrites stale complete state to prevent a failing build from passing the commit gate. STEP_HINT and workflow-gate.js SKILL_MAP hint updated. docs/architecture/claude-code/workflow.md table row updated. Direct Bash test path retained as backward-compatible fallback.

### BUGFIX: enforce-worktree: three main-worktree unblock fixes — push-from-main, interpreter-c read-only, orphan-dir cleanup (2026-05-13, pending)
Background: Three write-guard over-blocking cases after ENFORCE_WORKTREE hardening. (1) git push origin main was blocked even when all outgoing commit files were ENFORCE_WORKTREE_EXCLUDE-covered (docs-only PRs blocked). (2) bash -c read-only invocations were unconditionally write-classified, blocking planning skill config checks from the main worktree. (3) Orphan directory removal after git worktree remove was blocked by both the hook and settings.json deny rules; no safe alternative existed.
Changes: Fix 1: hooks/enforce-worktree.js: added resolveUpstream and isAllowedPushAllExcluded (fail-closed refspec parser using git log --name-only <upstream>..HEAD; pre-resolves file paths against repoRoot to guard against hook CWD != repoRoot). Fix 2: hooks/lib/bash-write-patterns.js: added isReadOnlyInterpreterC (guards: ANSI-C escapes, here-strings, here-docs, backtick subst, outer chaining, embedded newlines, depth-1 nested interpreter; segment-split classify-each); classify returns read when interpreter-c is the sole non-quoting match and body passes all guards. Fix 3: hooks/cleanup-orphan-dir.js NEW standalone script with 9-step validation pipeline (WORKTREE_BASE_DIR check, path.relative traversal guard, symlink rejection, registered-worktree check via git worktree list --porcelain, non-empty dir rejection, floor check); uses fs.rmdirSync. settings.json: two allow rules added for node hooks/cleanup-orphan-dir.js (POSIX + Windows forms). skills/worktree-end/SKILL.md step 6e: rewritten to use cleanup-orphan-dir.js instead of direct recursive delete from main. Tests: 33 cases across three new scripts — fix-enforce-worktree-push-fix-range.sh (14), fix-enforce-worktree-push-fix-interp.sh (11), fix-enforce-worktree-push-fix-cleanup.sh (8); all pass.


### FEATURE: GitHub Issues full migration — docs/todo.md to thin ID index (2026-05-13, pending)
Background: Direct commits to docs/todo.md caused merge conflicts across sessions and blocked push and worktree-end flows. GitHub Issues adopted as SSOT; todo.md reduced to a number index to eliminate conflicts.
Changes: Created 157 closed issues (#65-#221) from completed history entries (bin/github-issues/migration/migrate-history.sh). Created 24 open issues (#222-#245) from todo.md task sections (bin/github-issues/migration/migrate-todo.sh). Established Projects v2 'agents — Issue Timeline' with Content Date custom field; backfilled all 181 items (bin/github-issues/migration/backfill-content-date.sh). Rewrote docs/todo.md as a thin '- [ ] #N title' ID index. Added bin/github-issues/migration/preview-history.sh for dry-run preview. Reorganised issue scripts under bin/github-issues/ (ongoing) and bin/github-issues/migration/ (one-time).


### BUGFIX: show-diff.js: extend plan-file suppression from plans/drafts/ to all plans/ (2026-05-13, pending)
Background: The 2026-05-12 planning pipeline UX PR suppressed diff previews only for plans/drafts/, leaving final plan artifacts (*-intent.md, *-outline.md, *-detail.md) still triggering previews.
Changes: Renamed isPlanDraftFile to isPlanFile; widened path check from /.claude/plans/drafts/ to /.claude/plans/. Added tests/feature-show-diff-plan-file.sh (9 cases: T1-T9). skills/worktree-start/SKILL.md step 9e: added note that 'Symlink source rejected: .env' is expected when .env is a symlink in dotfiles setups.


### BUGFIX: pre-commit dotenv order fix + enforce-worktree hooks bypass block (2026-05-13, pending)
Background: Fix 1: hooks/pre-commit dotenv-add block ran before private-repo check, blocking .env commits on private repos. Fix 2: hooks/enforce-worktree.js lacked detection of git hooks bypass patterns.
Changes: Fix 1: swapped block order in hooks/pre-commit so private-repo check runs first. Fix 2: added hasGitHooksBypass() to hooks/enforce-worktree.js detecting core.hooksPath bypass via -c, --config-env, GIT_CONFIG_PARAMETERS, GIT_CONFIG_KEY_N. Tests: fix-pre-commit-dotenv-order.sh (7) + fix-enforce-worktree-hooks-bypass.sh (53); all pass.


### FEATURE: approve-plan-writes.js: auto-approve planning skill writes via PreToolUse hook (2026-05-13)
Background: VS Code Claude Code extension's ask-before-edits mode bypasses settings.json permissions.allow/deny path-based glob rules for Edit/Write tools entirely — confirmed empirically (7 candidate glob patterns, all BLOCK; also documented in 2026-05-04 SECURITY history entry). Planning skills (clarify-intent, make-outline-plan, make-detail-plan) write artifacts to ~/.claude/plans/, triggering repeated permission dialogs. The only reliable fix is a PreToolUse hook returning {"decision":"approve"} before the VS Code permission system fires.
Changes: Added hooks/lib/path-match.js: shared path-normalization library (normalizeSlashes, getBasename, getPathSegments, expandHome, isUnderPath; cross-platform, case-insensitive on win32). Added hooks/approve-plan-writes.js: PreToolUse hook that approves Write/Edit/MultiEdit targeting ~/.claude/plans/ by calling isUnderPath. Registered in settings.json PreToolUse after show-diff.js and before block-tests-direct.js. Created GitHub Issues #252/#253/#254; #252 and #254 as sub-issues of #253. Added tests/fix-windows-permission-glob.sh (40 cases).


### CONFIG: settings.json: remove 6 narrow drafts allow rules superseded by approve-plan-writes.js (2026-05-13)
Background: The 6 Write/Edit rules for ~/.claude/plans/drafts/** were confirmed dead on VS Code (ask-before-edits bypasses them) and are now fully covered by the approve-plan-writes.js PreToolUse hook.
Changes: Removed Write(~/.claude/plans/drafts/**), Edit(~/.claude/plans/drafts/**), and their **/ and Windows-backslash variants from settings.json permissions.allow. Updated tests/feature-allow-rules-planning.sh: removed NARROW_RULES presence assertions; added negative assertions confirming all 6 rules are absent.


### FEATURE: Migrate planning artifacts from ~/.claude/plans/ to ~/.workflow-plans/ (2026-05-14)
Background: ~/.claude/plans/ is a Claude Code protected path — VS Code ask-before-edits mode always prompts for Write/Edit calls under .claude/ regardless of PreToolUse hooks or settings.json allow rules (confirmed in prior session). The approve-plan-writes.js workaround could not fully suppress the dialogs. Moving artifacts to ~/.workflow-plans/ (outside the protected .claude/ tree) eliminates the root cause: all modes write silently, no hook needed.
Changes: New hooks/lib/workflow-plans-dir.js: getWorkflowPlansDir() reads WORKFLOW_PLANS_DIR env var (must be absolute; tilde/relative rejected), defaults to ~/.workflow-plans/. New bin/workflow-plans-dir bash bridge (cygpath-aware for Windows). New bin/migrate-plans-dir.js: two-pass content-aware migration (conflict detect → copy → verify → delete src); DST-in-SRC guard; run once on this machine. Deleted hooks/approve-plan-writes.js (no longer needed). Removed ~/.claude/plans/ allow rules from settings.json. Updated hooks/show-diff.js and hooks/workflow-gate.js to call getWorkflowPlansDir(). Updated bin/session-sync.sh and bin/session-sync.ps1 to resolve plans dir via helper. Updated skills/clarify-intent, make-outline-plan, make-detail-plan SKILL.md path references. Updated 15+ test files (path fixtures, removed approve-plan-writes integration tests). New tests: feature-workflow-plans-dir.sh (9 cases), feature-migrate-plans-dir.sh (6 cases).


### FEATURE: confirm-plan protocol + show-diff suppression narrowed to final artifacts only (2026-05-14, caa898d)
Background: Three CONFIRM_* steps (CONFIRM_INTENT, CONFIRM_OUTLINE, CONFIRM_DETAIL) each duplicated the same show-diff preview / flag-check / AskUserQuestion loop inline. show-diff.js suppressed all files under ~/.workflow-plans/ including final plan artifacts, preventing users from seeing intent.md, outline.md, and detail.md diffs in VS Code.
Changes: Added skills/_shared/confirm-plan.md as a shared protocol reference for all CONFIRM_* steps: step 1 (show-diff auto-preview fires on Write), step 2 (absolute path link), step 3 (flag check — OFF: proceed with summary; ON: AskUserQuestion with revise loop). skills/clarify-intent, make-outline-plan, make-detail-plan SKILL.md updated to reference the shared protocol instead of duplicating it. show-diff.js isPlanFile narrowed from all files under workflow-plans/ to only files under workflow-plans/drafts/; final artifacts now show diffs.


### REFACTOR: skills/_shared/: consolidate confirm-plan and test-design shared references (2026-05-14, 9593dce)
Background: confirm-plan-artifact/reference.md and test-design-shared/reference.md were separate directories with no consistent naming convention. The _archived/ precedent suggested using underscore-prefixed directories for non-executable shared content.
Changes: Moved skills/confirm-plan-artifact/reference.md to skills/_shared/confirm-plan.md and skills/test-design-shared/reference.md to skills/_shared/test-design.md. Updated references in skills/clarify-intent, make-outline-plan, make-detail-plan, write-tests, review-tests SKILL.md files and tests/refactor-rules-progressive-disclosure.sh. Deleted old directories.


### BUGFIX: issue-to-history.sh: fix --date missing, --issue removed, --commits injection, FEATURE idempotency (2026-05-14, #261)
Background: issue-to-history.sh had four bugs: (1) --date was not passed to doc-append, so header date fell back to doc-append's internal today instead of the issue's closedAt; (2) --issue N was passed but doc-append does not accept that flag, causing a silent parse-skip; (3) --commit (singular) was used but doc-append only recognises --commits; (4) the FEATURE idempotency regex only matched the legacy ### #N: prefix, missing entries with the new parenthetical (date, #N) format. Also, closedAt was not requested from the GitHub API.
Changes: Added closedAt to gh issue view --json field list and derived CLOSED_DATE from it. Removed --issue from ARGS; added --date "$CLOSED_DATE". Renamed --commit to --commits and injected #N as "${COMMIT}, #${ISSUE_NUM}" or "#${ISSUE_NUM}" so the issue number is embedded in the history header without modifying doc-append. Updated idempotency regex to OR-match both legacy ### #N: and new ### [^(]+ (..., #N) forms. Fixed skip message. Updated tests/fixtures/gh-mock/doc-append to parse --date; updated tests/feature-issue-migration-issue-close.sh (N1/I1/I2 updated + N5-N9 added; 15/15 pass).


### FEATURE: clarify-intent: auto-detect closes_issues; CLAUDE.md: Step 10 issue-close reminder (2026-05-14, #257)
Background: clarify-intent had no mechanism to record which GitHub issue a session was closing, so /issue-close had to be invoked manually after the workflow without session context. CLAUDE.md Step 10 had no reminder to call /issue-close.
Changes: skills/clarify-intent/SKILL.md: added step 1a to auto-detect #N tokens in the user's initial message; records the issue number as closes_issues in intent.md without asking when unambiguous (single #N + clear context), asks during the interview when ambiguous, sets empty list when none found. Added closes_issues field to intent.md Output Schema with serialization examples. CLAUDE.md: added paragraph after Step 10 listing closes_issues usage and /issue-close <N> invocation pattern.

### BUGFIX: confirm-plan protocol: enforce mandatory Steps 1+2 and fix make-outline-plan double-write (2026-05-14, 87fc8de)
Background: Agents executing the planning workflow would sometimes skip the clickable artifact link (confirm-plan protocol Step 2) in OFF mode, because the shared protocol's three-step requirement was under-specified. In make-outline-plan, the file write was split across Step 7 (OFF path) and Step 8 with no protocol coverage in the SINGLE_APPROACH branch. The Rule section said 'One AskUserQuestion per run', which was incorrect for ON mode where approach-selection (Step 7) and artifact-review (Step 8) are distinct touchpoints.
Changes: skills/_shared/confirm-plan.md rewritten: three mandatory steps labelled explicitly; Step 2 marked MANDATORY with 'before any chat output that follows the write' ordering (tool calls allowed between); Step 3 OFF no longer duplicates the link. skills/make-outline-plan/SKILL.md: SINGLE_APPROACH branch now applies full confirm-plan protocol Steps 1+2+3; file write moved from Step 7 to Step 8; Step 8 applies full protocol; Rules section updated to 'Two AskUserQuestion calls per run in ON mode' with rationale. skills/make-detail-plan/SKILL.md: OFF path note updated to reference protocol Steps 2+3.


### BUGFIX: enforce-worktree: move /worktree-end marker out of .git/ to eliminate protected-path permission prompts (2026-05-14, #270)
Background: The pending-branch-delete marker written by /worktree-end was stored at <git-common-dir>/info/pending-branch-delete — inside .git/, a Claude Code protected path. In default and acceptEdits modes, writes always trigger a permission prompt regardless of settings.json allow rules. The existing Write/**/.git/info/pending-branch-delete allow rule was therefore ineffective. Issue #270. Same root cause as the .claude/plans/ → ~/.workflow-plans/ fix in PR #256.
Changes: Moved marker to ~/.workflow-plans/worktree-end/pending-branch-delete-<sha256-16>--<encoded-branch> (WORKFLOW_PLANS_DIR customisable). Added getWorktreeEndDir(), getRepoId() (sha256 .slice(0,16), 64-bit repo ID from git-common-dir), and getMarkerPath() helpers to enforce-worktree.js. Rewrote isAllowedBranchDeleteViaMarker (uses getMarkerPath directly), isAllowedMarkerDelete (validates directory + sha256-16 repo-id prefix + git show-ref branch-gone check; fs.readFileSync(nTarget) for path consistency), and isMarkerFilePath (validates directory + sha256-16 prefix + non-empty branch suffix). Security follow-up: sha1 → sha256, readFileSync(target) → readFileSync(nTarget). Updated skills/worktree-end/SKILL.md Steps 6b (repo-id computation, encoded-branch, marker-path storage) and 6g (reuse <marker-path>, not re-derive from WORKFLOW_PLANS_DIR). Deleted 3 dead allow rules for .git/info path from settings.json. Updated tests/fix-settings-marker-cleanup.sh: marker_path_for(repo,branch) computes sha256-16 path, per-test WORKFLOW_PLANS_DIR isolation, T19-T21 removed (no longer relevant), T23 renamed to isMarkerFilePath_wrong_repo_id; 24 numbered tests pass.


### FEATURE: issue-fix-history cross-reference — closes #N PR flow, auto_close_path, backfill (#222) (2026-05-14)
Background: Issues closed via GitHub's closes #N merge keyword left no history.md entry and no resolved-by sentinel comment. /issue-close aborted on CLOSED+no-sentinel state instead of recovering gracefully. No mechanism existed to retroactively link past closed issues to their commit hashes.
Changes: New bin/github-issues/issue-close-triage.sh encodes all state×sentinel routing (OPEN/CLOSED × none/pending/appended → NEXT_STEPS); CLOSED+no-sentinel maps to auto_close_path (B,E,G,J). New bin/github-issues/post-close-sentinels.sh: J-1 resolved-by hash comment (when --commit provided), J-2 appended sentinel — both idempotent via gh --jq. New bin/github-issues/parent-body-update.sh: flips - [ ] #N → - [x] #N in parent body, no-op when no parent. New bin/github-issues/backfill-commit-comments.sh: --dry-run mode; posts appended sentinel with optional commit hash extracted from history.md for all closed issues lacking one. skills/issue-close/SKILL.md rewritten to 103 lines (was 177); all state routing delegated to triage script; Step J added. rules/github-issues.md: dual-write period ended; docs/todo.md is now a pointer only; two close flows documented. skills/commit-push/SKILL.md: PR step instructs inclusion of Closes #N in --body. CLAUDE.md: noted that /issue-close handles CLOSED+(none) state after PR auto-close.

### FEATURE: Add /workflow-init routing skill — issue-aware workflow entry point (Path A/B/C) (2026-05-14, pending)
Background: clarify-intent was always the mandatory first step, but GitHub issue integration required routing at session start: sessions with an issue marked intent:clarified should skip the interview entirely; sessions with an issue but no label should pre-fill the interview; sessions with no issue should run the full interview and auto-create a tracking issue at completion.
Changes: Added skills/workflow-init/SKILL.md: three routing paths (A: intent:clarified label -> write intent.md + skip interview; B: issue present, no label -> write pre-fill file + delegate to clarify-intent; C: no issue -> delegate to clarify-intent as before). Added workflow_init as first element of VALID_STEPS in hooks/lib/workflow-state.js; migration backfills legacy sessions (ci complete/skipped -> wi complete; ci absent or pending -> wi pending). Added STEP_HINT entry for workflow_init. workflow-gate.js: replaced single-tier early gate with two-tier check (Tier 1: workflow_init, Tier 2: clarify_intent); plans-path allowlist moved outside both tiers; SKILL_MAP updated. hooks/session-start.js: fallback NEXT_ACTION changed from clarify_intent to workflow_init hint. skills/clarify-intent/SKILL.md: added step 1a auto-detect closes_issues note that pre-fill satisfies issue detection; added step 1b pre-fill detection from ~/.workflow-plans/drafts/<sid>-issue-prefill.md; Completion section updated with issue reconciliation (label N -> add intent:clarified; empty -> gh issue create + update closes_issues); TodoWrite checklist now marks workflow_init + clarify_intent completed. CLAUDE.md Step 1 replaced with /workflow-init routing description. .github/labels.yml: appended intent:clarified label (color 1d76db). tests/feature-workflow-init-routing.sh: 22 cases covering migration (M1-M4), early gate (G5-G8), sentinel (S9), and content checks (C10-C12).

### FEATURE: show-plan-link.js: PostToolUse hook to emit plan file path breadcrumb (2026-05-15)
Background: confirm-plan.md Step 2 instructed the LLM to print a clickable absolute-path link after each plan artifact write, but execution was unreliable — tildes unexpanded, Windows backslashes used, and VS Code extension webview upstream bug (issues #53919, #16056) preventing clickable links on C: drive paths. Moving this to a deterministic PostToolUse hook ensures the breadcrumb always appears.
Changes: New hooks/show-plan-link.js: fires on Write tool for files directly under ~/.workflow-plans/ matching *-(intent|outline|detail).md (not drafts/); emits {systemMessage:"Plan file written: <abs-path>"}; when CLAUDE_CODE_ENTRYPOINT==="claude-vscode" also spawns code -r <path> fire-and-forget via cmd.exe on Windows (CVE-2024-27980 workaround) or directly on POSIX. Registered in settings.json as PostToolUse Write matcher. skills/_shared/confirm-plan.md Step 2 rewritten: hook handles breadcrumb; fallback to plain-text if hook not deployed; removed markdown link instructions. New tests/feature-show-plan-link.sh: 19 cases (T1-T19) covering artifact detection, exclusions, tool filtering, Windows backslash normalization, VS Code shim, custom WORKFLOW_PLANS_DIR, malformed stdin, and failed-write detection.


### BUGFIX: enforce-issue-close: inline ISSUE_CLOSE_SKILL=1 bypass + worktree dead-CWD fail-open + /worktree-end CWD step (#267, #268, #251) (2026-05-15, pending)
Background: Three linked bugs. (1) #267: enforce-issue-close.js only checked process.env.ISSUE_CLOSE_SKILL — missed inline VAR=val cmd prefix form used by /issue-close skill. (2) #268: after /worktree-end removed the linked worktree, subsequent hook invocations ran with a dead CWD, breaking enforce-worktree.js for the rest of the session. (3) #251: Windows OS CWD lock caused git worktree remove EPERM when session CWD was inside the worktree being removed.
Changes: (1) Added INLINE_SKILL_RE strict-shape regex to enforce-issue-close.js; matches only the exact canonical invocation shape the skill generates (ISSUE_CLOSE_SKILL=1 as inline prefix, digits-only issue id, --reason completed required, end-anchored, horizontal whitespace only). Tests N11-N21 added to feature-issue-migration-enforce-hook.sh. (2) Added dead-CWD fail-open to enforce-worktree.js: process.cwd() ENOENT catch-and-done + fs.existsSync false branch-and-done; both paths emit audit stderr. Tests C1-C5 in new fix-enforce-worktree-cwd-fallback.sh. (3) Inserted step 6b.5 in skills/worktree-end/SKILL.md: standalone cd to main-worktree-root as a separate Bash call before step 6c worktree remove (chained form blocked by enforce-worktree.js isolated-command rule).

Changes: Added .github/workflows/sync-labels.yml — triggers on push to main touching labels.yml (plus workflow_dispatch). Shortened intent:clarified description to fit GitHub's 100-char limit. Closes #278.

### FEATURE: worktree-start / worktree-end hidden from slash-command autocomplete (#281) (2026-05-15, pending)
Background: /workflow-init required typing 'workf' (5 chars) to uniquely complete because worktree-start and worktree-end shared the 'work' prefix. Users never invoke these skills directly — they are workflow-internal only.

Claude Code's documented frontmatter field hides the skill from the / autocomplete menu while keeping Skill-tool invocation intact. Result: 'wo' + Enter now uniquely completes to /workflow-init.

Background: /workflow-init required typing 'workf' (5 chars) to uniquely complete because worktree-start and worktree-end shared the 'work' prefix. Users never invoke these skills directly — they are workflow-internal only.
