### FEATURE: review-skill-size — Step 6 skill size/quality review script (#284) (2026-05-15)
Background: CC tends to write verbose skill definitions. Added an automated size/quality check to Step 6 so that any session editing a SKILL.md file gets a non-blocking review.
Changes: Added bin/review-skill-size — a bash script that detects changed skills/*/SKILL.md files (excluding _archived/), warns when a file exceeds 100 lines, and always prints a qualitative checklist for checks 2/3/4 (remove verbose prose, extract complex logic to external scripts, split overscoped skills). Integrated into CLAUDE.md Step 6 as a parallel Bash call (review-skill-size --base <merge-base>). Added tests/feature-review-skill-size.sh with 7 smoke test cases. Closes #284.


### FEATURE: review-skill-size — Step 6 skill size/quality review script (#284) (2026-05-15)
Background: CC tends to write verbose skill definitions. Added an automated size/quality check to Step 6 so that any session editing a SKILL.md file gets a non-blocking review.
Changes: Added bin/review-skill-size — a bash script that detects changed skills/*/SKILL.md files (excluding _archived/), warns when a file exceeds 100 lines, and always prints a qualitative checklist for checks 2/3/4 (remove verbose prose, extract complex logic to external scripts, split overscoped skills). Integrated into CLAUDE.md Step 6 as a parallel Bash call (review-skill-size --base <merge-base>). Added tests/feature-review-skill-size.sh with 7 smoke test cases. Closes #284.

### FEATURE: Add /issue-create skill — type:task enforcement + Projects v2 auto-attach (#246) (2026-05-15)
Background: Sessions needed a sanctioned CLI path for creating task issues that enforces type:task and attaches to Projects v2 automatically. Raw gh issue create risked wrong labels and manual project attachment.
Changes: New bin/github-issues/issue-create.sh: wraps gh issue create; unconditionally applies type:task; rejects caller-supplied type:* labels (exit 2); supports --body/--body-file (mutually exclusive); Projects v2 attach non-fatal on failure (ISSUE_CREATE_OWNER/ISSUE_CREATE_PROJECT_NUM env overrides). New skills/issue-create/SKILL.md: user-invocable skill with scope, pre-flight, procedure, label policy. Updated rules/github-issues.md: added Issue creation section. Updated CLAUDE.md: added /issue-create note under Step 1. New tests/feature-issue-create-skill.sh: 15 cases (S1-S11 behavior + D1-D4 docs).


### FEATURE: Switch VS Code detection in show-plan-link.js to TERM_PROGRAM (2026-05-15, pending)
Background: CLAUDE_CODE_ENTRYPOINT is a Claude Code internal variable not guaranteed by Anthropic public API. TERM_PROGRAM=vscode is the POSIX-standard signal set by VS Code's integrated terminal. closes #290.
Changes: hooks/show-plan-link.js: gate on TERM_PROGRAM === 'vscode' instead of CLAUDE_CODE_ENTRYPOINT === 'claude-vscode'; updated NOTE comment. tests/feature-show-plan-link.sh: T16 driven by TERM_PROGRAM=vscode; added T20 (non-VS Code negative), T21 (legacy var regression guard), T22 (fail-open with failing code shim); default unset extended. docs/architecture/claude-code/settings.md: updated description.


### FEATURE: backfill-commit-comments.sh: J-1 + git-log fallback + --canary flag (#300) (2026-05-15, pending)
Background: Closed issues lacking J-1/J-2 comments (issues closed via web UI, 'closes #N' PR keyword, or before /issue-close was in place) had no human-readable 'Resolved by commit' comment or machine-readable sentinel. backfill-commit-comments.sh only posted a J-2 sentinel when a history.md entry existed, and warned+skipped otherwise.
Changes: Enhanced backfill-commit-comments.sh with 3-tier hash discovery (Tier 1: history.md heading bracket hex, Tier 2: git log --all --grep boundary-safe pattern preventing #42 from matching #420, Tier 3: no-hash). Added J-1 'Resolved by commit' posting with has_resolved_by() idempotency check. Added --canary flag (bash 3.2 compatible scalars, 1 issue per class max 3 total). Class-specific J-2 sentinel bodies (backfill / backfill-gitlog / backfill-no-hash). Issues missing from history.md now fall through to git-log then no-hash J-2 instead of warn+skip. Added ops.md self-contained migration runbook. Tests: R4 updated + R5-R12 added (40 total, all pass).


### FEATURE: Add cc-session-title CLI — VS Code session title auto-update from workflow (#299) (2026-05-15, pending)
Background: The VS Code Claude Code extension displays a session title in the sidebar, but it never reflected which issue was being worked on — sessions always showed the auto-generated title. The update mechanism is a custom-title JSONL record appended to ~/.claude/projects/<encoded-cwd>/<session>.jsonl; the extension picks up the latest record on next render. CLAUDE_SESSION_ID is unavailable in Bash subprocesses (Anthropic bug #27987), so the current session file is discovered via mtime.
Changes: New bin/cc-session-title.py: set-issue <N> "<title>" appends a custom-title record to the newest JSONL in the project dir; add-pr and mark-complete are stubbed (Phase 2/3). Manual-rename guard: skips update when latest custom-title does not match ^(#\d+\s|✓). Installers: cc-session-title launcher added to install/linux/dotfileslink.sh; cmd + bash shim added to install/win/dotfileslink.ps1. Skills: workflow-init steps A2.5 and B2.5 call cc-session-title set-issue immediately after fetching issue title; clarify-intent Completion substeps 2 and 3 call cc-session-title set-issue after labelling/creating the issue. Tests: 38 pytest cases in tests/test_feat-cc-session-title.py covering encode_cwd, latest_custom_title, is_workflow_generated, build_record, format_set_issue_title, run_set_issue integration, and main stubs.


### BUGFIX: enforce-worktree CWD issues: cd+worktree-remove, post-cleanup guard, FD redirect, orphan-dir path (#242, #243, #294, #297) (2026-05-15)
Background: Four linked bugs blocked worktree-end cleanup operations and planning workflow commands from the main worktree. Root cause: enforce-worktree.js lacked exemptions for (a) the combined cd+git-worktree-remove pattern needed when VS Code resets Bash CWD between calls (#294), (b) stash/restore/checkout after all linked worktrees are removed (#297), (c) read-only planning commands using 2>&1 or 1>&2 FD-to-FD redirects falsely classified as writes (#243), and (d) cleanup-orphan-dir.js invoked via relative path that breaks when CWD resets to a deleted directory (#242).
Changes: hooks/enforce-worktree.js: added findFirstUnquotedAnd(cmd) — quote-aware && scanner used by new isAllowedCdWorktreeRemove; added isAllowedCdWorktreeRemove(cmd, repoRoot) — allows exactly one unquoted &&, LHS must cd to main worktree, RHS must be git worktree remove|prune with no --force/-f, multiple -C flags rejected; added isAllowedMainWorktreeCleanup(cmd, repoRoot) — allows stash push|pop|apply|drop|clear, restore (no --source), checkout path-restore (requires -- separator, no -b/-B/-f) gated on git worktree list --porcelain returning exactly 1 entry (main-only). hooks/lib/bash-write-patterns.js: posix-redirect regex adds (?!&\d) lookahead after >>? to exclude FD-to-FD redirects (2>&1, 1>&2) from write classification; header comment updated to note the fix. skills/worktree-end/SKILL.md: step 6e changed from relative node hooks/cleanup-orphan-dir.js to absolute node "C:\git\agents/hooks/cleanup-orphan-dir.js"; step 6b.5 note updated to document that the combined cd+worktree-remove form is now permitted via isAllowedCdWorktreeRemove. Three new test files: fix-bash-write-patterns-fd-redirect.sh (7 cases), fix-enforce-worktree-main-cleanup.sh (27 cases), fix-enforce-worktree-cd-worktree-remove.sh (13 cases). Closes #242, #243, #294, #297; parent issue #296 closed after merge.


### BUGFIX: backfill-commit-comments.sh: drop unsupported --paginate flag (#300 hotfix) (2026-05-15, pending)
Background: After PR #301 merged the new backfill-commit-comments.sh and the dry-run was executed, it processed 0 of 175 closed issues. Root cause: gh issue list does not support --paginate (only gh api does); the script's gh issue list --state closed --limit 1000 --paginate ... call printed an error to stderr that was swallowed by 2>/dev/null, leaving ALL_NUMBERS empty. Tests passed because the gh mock dispatch pattern also matched on --paginate, masking the real-CLI incompatibility.
Changes: Drop --paginate from the gh issue list call (--limit 1000 is sufficient for foreseeable closed-issue counts; consumer repos with >1000 closed issues can use gh api workflow). Update gh mock dispatch pattern to match --limit instead of --paginate so tests stay aligned with real-CLI behavior. Tests: 40 pass.


### BUGFIX: tracking: worktree-end x CWD issues parent (#296) (2026-05-15, 396a534, #296)
Background: Parent tracking issue grouping four enforce-worktree CWD bugs (#242 orphan-dir path, #243 FD-redirect false-positive, #294 cd+worktree-remove combined form, #297 post-cleanup stash/restore/checkout) blocking worktree-end and planning workflows from the main worktree.
Changes: All four child issues resolved by PR #304 (squash commit 396a534). See main BUGFIX entry for #242, #243, #294, #297 for implementation details.


### BUGFIX: planning skills: CONFIRM_* checks blocked by FD-redirect false-positive in bash-write-patterns.js (#243) (2026-05-15, 396a534, #243)
Background: Read-only planning commands using 2 and 1 FD-to-FD redirects were falsely classified as writes by bash-write-patterns.js, blocking skill steps such as CONFIRM_* flag checks from the main worktree.
Changes: posix-redirect regex in hooks/lib/bash-write-patterns.js: added negative-lookahead for FD-to-FD redirects so that 2-to-1 and 1-to-2 forms are excluded from write classification. Part of BUGFIX entry for #242, #243, #294, #297.


### BUGFIX: enforce-worktree: allow cd+git-worktree-remove combined form for VS Code CWD-reset behavior (#294) (2026-05-15, 396a534, #294)
Background: enforce-worktree.js lacked an exemption for the cd-main-then-git-worktree-remove pattern, required when VS Code extension host resets the Bash CWD between calls during worktree-end cleanup step 6b.5.
Changes: Added findFirstUnquotedAnd() and isAllowedCdWorktreeRemove() to hooks/enforce-worktree.js. Part of BUGFIX entry for #242, #243, #294, #297.


### CONFIG: Disable 1M context for skill/subagent calls (CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_1M_CONTEXT) (2026-05-16)
Background: Skill/agent frontmatter with model: opus or model: sonnet resolves to 1M-context defaults (Opus 4.7 and Sonnet 4.6 are natively 1M), causing frequent 'Extra usage is required for 1M context' API errors when Sonnet 1M is not covered by the active plan. The UI-level non-1M model selection constrains only the main session, not subagents.
Changes: Added CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_1M_CONTEXT=1 to the env block in settings.json. This env var (officially added in Claude Code changelog) forces all sessions and subagent invocations to use standard-context (200K) mode, preventing alias resolution from silently upgrading to 1M.


### BUGFIX: fix Tier 1 hash extraction in backfill-commit-comments.sh (2026-05-16, 2749dce, #307)
Background: backfill-commit-comments.sh dry-run showed hash-from-history: 0 out of 161 closed issues. Two independent bugs in discover_hash_from_history(): (1) grep pattern [,)] missing : so INCIDENT: #N: and BUGFIX: #N: headings never matched; (2) tail -n 1 selected only the last paren group, missing hashes in earlier bracket positions.
Changes: Fix A: extend [,)] to [,):] in both grep patterns (lines 46, 48) so category-prefixed INCIDENT/BUGFIX headings match. Fix B: remove tail -n 1 from the bracket-scan pipeline so all paren groups are scanned for a 7-40 hex match. Comment updated from 'last bracket group' to 'any bracket group'. Regression tests R13/R14/R15 added to tests/feature-issue-222.sh covering the archive path and both fixes independently. Closes #307.


### FEATURE: Projects v2 Content Date backfill and issue-create auto-set (2026-05-16)
Background: Projects v2 'Content Date' field was missing for 14 issues (#246, #247, #249, #250, #251, #257, #261, #262, #267, #268, #283, #299, #300, #307) created in the new format. The existing backfill-content-date.sh only handles the old history-entry format.
Changes: One-off backfill: 14/14 issues set via gh project item-edit using createdAt date (node one-liner, no migration script). issue-create.sh: attach call now uses --format json --jq '.id' to capture item_id and calls gh project item-edit --date with createdAt[:10]; ISSUE_CREATE_FIELD_ID / ISSUE_CREATE_PROJECT_ID env-var overrides added. SKILL.md: documented Content Date auto-set and new env vars. tests/feature-issue-create-skill.sh: T1-T4 added.


### FEATURE: #262: plan-time premise verification gate (2026-05-16, 37e5314)
Background: PR #311 implements the plan-time premise verification gate requested in #262. Root cause: planning pipeline ran on stated issue premises without verifying real-tool behavior, leading to wasted work (#250, #261). Added two-axis research verification: (1) /survey-code now extracts and verifies up to 5 behavioral claims from intent.md against live code; (2) new /survey-history skill checks git log + docs/history.md + archived entries + GitHub PRs since the issue opened for changes that contradict issue premises. make-outline-plan Step 0 aggregates both artifacts and emits WORKFLOW_PREMISE_FAIL if contradictions found, prompting user with AskUserQuestion before proceeding.
Changes: Added skills/survey-history/SKILL.md and agents/survey-history.md (new subagent); strengthened skills/survey-code/SKILL.md with Step 0 claim extraction and Step 5 verified-claims output; added WORKFLOW_PREMISE_FAIL/ACK sentinels to hooks/workflow-mark.js and settings.json; added Step 0 aggregation block to skills/make-outline-plan/SKILL.md; added setPremiseContradiction/clearPremiseContradiction/getPremiseContradiction to hooks/lib/workflow-state.js; updated agents/outline-planner.md with sentinel-emission constraint.


### FEATURE: Unify plan-artifact path presentation: SKILL.md and hook now mandate Windows backslash absolute path (2026-05-16, 5988ab1, #314)
Background: After make-outline-plan finished, the orchestrator was generating its own confirmation text (e.g. 'outline.md written: ~/.workflow-plans/...(full path: C:/Users/.../...)') which was neither clickable nor consistently formatted. Root cause: skills/_shared/confirm-plan.md had been aggregated as the single source of truth, but stale '(b) the final clickable link' instructions remained in skills/make-outline-plan/SKILL.md and skills/make-detail-plan/SKILL.md, so the agent kept producing custom strings. Two adjacent gaps were folded in: show-plan-link.js auto-open relied on TERM_PROGRAM only and never fired in the VS Code extension webview, and the WORKFLOW_USER_VERIFIED step gave no visibility into staged files or PR URL.
Changes: (PR1) Removed stale clickable-link instructions from make-outline-plan/SKILL.md and make-detail-plan/SKILL.md, delegating fully to confirm-plan.md. Hardened confirm-plan.md Step 2 with an explicit prohibition list (no duplicate, translate, paraphrase, markdown link, tilde, file:/// URI, dual-path annotation, Japanese sentence-prepend). show-plan-link.js now emits Windows backslash absolute paths (POSIX keeps forward slashes). Added Windows backslash output test in feature-show-plan-link.sh. (PR2) Extracted shouldOpenInVsCode() with layered detection (TERM_PROGRAM=vscode OR CLAUDE_CODE_ENTRYPOINT=claude-vscode) plus SHOW_PLAN_LINK_NO_AUTO_OPEN=1 opt-out; documented VSCODE_IPC_HOOK_CLI rejection rationale; added 5 layer-detection tests and inverted T21. New hooks/show-user-verified-context.js detects WORKFLOW_USER_VERIFIED in tool_input.command and emits a systemMessage listing staged files and the open PR URL; registered the PostToolUse Bash matcher in settings.json; added feature-show-user-verified.sh (11 cases, U5 skipped on Windows due to Node spawnSync limitation with POSIX-format PATH inherited from Git-Bash).


### BUGFIX: fix: cc-session-title set-issue unconditional overwrite (#299) (2026-05-16, 95230ad)
Background: run_set_issue had an is_workflow_generated guard that blocked title writes when Claude Code auto-titler set the session title first. Auto-titler titles never match the workflow regex, causing a permanent no-op that made the entire title chain ineffective.
Changes: Removed is_workflow_generated guard from run_set_issue. set-issue now unconditionally writes the workflow title. add-pr and mark-complete guards are preserved.

### BUGFIX: #326: suppress VS Code spawn in tests via SHOW_PLAN_LINK_NO_SPAWN (2026-05-16, 927d40a)
Background: T16 and T22 in tests/feature-show-plan-link.sh launched real VS Code on Windows during test runs. Root cause: the hook uses spawn(cmd.exe, [/d, /s, /c, code, -r, absPath]) on Windows; cmd.exe resolves code via PATHEXT (.COM;.EXE;.BAT;.CMD) and ignores extensionless bash shims placed at the front of PATH, falling back to the real code.cmd and opening abc-detail.md in the user VS Code window.
Changes: Added SHOW_PLAN_LINK_NO_SPAWN=1 test-only env var bypass to openInVsCode() in hooks/show-plan-link.js. When set, the real code -r spawn is skipped; optional SHOW_PLAN_LINK_MARKER_FILE env var records invocation via a marker file for test assertion. Updated T16 and T22 to use SHOW_PLAN_LINK_NO_SPAWN=1 instead of PATH shim tricks, added T23 verifying the bypass block is not entered when only SHOW_PLAN_LINK_MARKER_FILE is set without SHOW_PLAN_LINK_NO_SPAWN. Closes #326.


### REFACTOR: #327: shift survey-code/survey-history left into workflow-init (2026-05-16, pending)
Background: The clarify-intent interview ran without codebase or history context — survey-code and survey-history only executed after the interview completed (clarify-intent Step 6 / Completion Step 3). This meant CC could not detect wrong premises or surface 'already done' facts during the interview, causing rework. Industry analysis (Devin/Cursor/Cline/Aider/OpenHands/SWE-agent) confirmed that code survey before or interleaved with interview is the canonical pattern.
Changes: workflow-init now writes a context.md fact-sheet (session-id, path, issue body sentinel-stripped, user prompt, issue metadata, top-20 keywords) and launches survey-code + survey-history as parallel Agent subagents on all Paths A/B/C before the interview. survey-code/SKILL.md Step 0: accepts context.md as fallback input when intent.md is not yet available (intent.md preferred post-clarify-intent). survey-history/SKILL.md Step 1: same input precedence; new Step 2.5 keyword-only mode (DEGRADED MODE header, all verdicts indeterminate, --since=1 year ago, skip gh pr list) when no issue number is available. clarify-intent Steps 6 and Completion 3: removed survey invocations; replaced with artifact-existence check — falls back to invoking missing surveys if workflow-init agents failed. New agents/survey-code.md (symmetric to agents/survey-history.md). Closes #327.


### FEATURE: Session-scoped ENFORCE_WORKTREE escape hatch (2026-05-17, 678016c, #336)
Background: ENFORCE_WORKTREE=on (default) blocks main-worktree writes. Maintenance scenarios such as worktree-end CWD-lock recovery (#296) sometimes need a temporary off, but editing .env globally leaks to other sessions and disrupts their worktree-start/end behavior.
Changes: Added two sentinels: WORKFLOW_ENFORCE_WORKTREE_OFF[: <reason>] writes a per-session marker (<workflowDir>/<sessionId>.worktree-off, atomic tmp+rename), and WORKFLOW_ENFORCE_WORKTREE_ON deletes it (idempotent, ENOENT no-op). workflow-mark.js (PostToolUse) handles ingestion; enforce-worktree.js (PreToolUse) checks the marker after isEnforceWorktreeOn() and allows writes when present. Session IDs validated with /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$/ before any fs operation; session-ID resolution via resolveSessionId() bypasses Anthropic bug #27987 (CLAUDE_SESSION_ID not propagated to Bash subprocesses). 25 tests across ingestion (A1-A11), guard consumption (B1-B5), round-trip (C1-C3), and security (SEC1-SEC5: path traversal, shell-metachar injection, env-file traversal).


### SECURITY: Require ask for WORKFLOW_ENFORCE_WORKTREE_OFF sentinel (security hotfix) (2026-05-17, pending, #336)
Background: Initial #336 implementation shipped WORKFLOW_ENFORCE_WORKTREE_OFF without an ask rule in settings.json. The sentinel fell through the generic echo allow path, so any CC session could silently lift main-worktree enforcement and bypass ENFORCE_WORKTREE=on without user confirmation. Discovered immediately after #336 merge when a CC session (this one) used the sentinel to commit docs/history.md from main with zero user prompt.
Changes: settings.json permissions.ask: added Bash(echo "<<WORKFLOW_ENFORCE_WORKTREE_OFF*>>"). ON sentinel left as auto-allow since restoring enforcement is the safe direction.


### BUGFIX: Orthogonalize ask sentinel rules — bare+reason coverage (#341) (2026-05-17)
Background: ask sentinel rules for ENFORCE_WORKTREE_OFF had a glob pattern (OFF*>>) that did not match the colon-containing reason form, so no ask prompt fired. Orthogonality audit revealed all sentinels in the ask array had inconsistent bare/reason coverage.
Changes: Split every ask entry into bare and reason forms; extended USER_VERIFIED and ENFORCE_WORKTREE_ON regexes to accept optional reason; fixed show-user-verified-context.js detection regex.


### BUGFIX: Tier 1.5 bulk-import commit blacklist in backfill-commit-comments.sh (#344) (2026-05-17, pending)
Background: Tier 1.5 (`git log --all --reverse -S "<title>"`) returns the oldest commit that introduced a history.md entry. For issues whose entries were bulk-imported in a single commit (`3969773 = feat(agents-split): add 39 tests from dotfiles (step 11)`), all affected issues resolved to the same import commit, causing false attribution. Discovered during M1 dry-run analysis; `3969773` accounted for 12–39 incorrectly attributed issues.
Changes: Added `TIER15_BLACKLIST="3969773"` constant and a prefix-match loop in `discover_hash_from_history_introducer()` — when `git log -S` resolves to a blacklisted hash, the function returns 1 and the classifier falls through to Tier 2 or no-hash. Added R26 (blacklist → no-hash) and R29 (blacklist + Tier 2 fallback) to `tests/feature-issue-222.sh`. 57/57 tests green. Proper Tier 1.5 refinement (collision detection, heuristic) tracked in #344.


### FEATURE: user-verified: PreToolUse hook migration so context surfaces before permission dialog (#318) (2026-05-17, da113d6)
Background: Under ENFORCE_WORKTREE=off step 8, when the user denied the sentinel approval, fixed the diff, and re-approved, the relevant URL had often scrolled out of view. Directly emitting the sentinel without an AskUserQuestion also made the link between the URL and the user's approval intent weak.
Changes: Migrated show-user-verified-context.js from PostToolUse to PreToolUse so staged files and Open PR URL are surfaced as a systemMessage immediately before the permission dialog. Added skills/_shared/user-verified.md as the shared protocol and made CLAUDE.md step 8 and worktree-end steps 3b/4 delegate to it. Dropped documentation of the bare <<WORKFLOW_USER_VERIFIED>> form — only the reason-bearing form is documented now. Rejected the AskUserQuestion approach: the permission dialog itself remains the load-bearing wall (tool-enforced, not protocol-only).


### FEATURE: Update README workflow mermaid chart (#348) (2026-05-17, 49294c1)
Background: The mermaid flowchart in README.md ('Hook-enforced end-to-end workflow') had not been updated since commit 76d4f8b (2026-05-10). Five workflow changes introduced by subsequent commits were missing from the chart.
Changes: Added S0 (workflow-init) as Step 0 before clarify-intent (DocCheck -- No --> S0 --> S1). Relabeled P2a as fallback since surveys now run in parallel inside workflow-init. Added S6d (review-skill-size) as a fourth parallel lane to the Review subgraph. Added IC (/issue-close --from-session) as a convergence node after all three Cleanup paths. Updated S5 label to reflect both ENFORCE_WORKTREE modes (on: Edit direct / off: diff to approve).


### FEATURE: WORKFLOW_PLANS_DIR: skills hardcode path, ignore env var (2026-05-17, d294414, #349)
Background: Skills hardcoded ~/.workflow-plans/ or used ${WORKFLOW_PLANS_DIR:-...} shell expansion, but LLM tool args (Read/Write/Edit/subagent prompts) are never piped through a shell — the brace expansion never fired and the override was silently ignored. JS hooks (loadDefaultEnv) and session-sync scripts (bin/workflow-plans-dir) honored the override, creating split behavior across the codebase.
Changes: Adopted orchestrator-injects pattern: each consuming SKILL.md inlines a Step 0 preamble that resolves PLANS_DIR once via bash bin/workflow-plans-dir, then substitutes the literal absolute path for every <PLANS_DIR> placeholder in subsequent tool args and subagent prompts. Added skills/_shared/resolve-plans-dir.md (canonical reference, inlined verbatim — no auto-load dependency). Added tests/feature-skills-plans-dir-drift-guard.sh and extended tests/feature-workflow-plans-dir.sh (I4/I5/I6). 8 SKILL.md files updated: workflow-init, clarify-intent, survey-code, survey-history, make-outline-plan, make-detail-plan, worktree-end, issue-close.


### REFACTOR: Rename plan-principles → core-principles, add §4 SSOT, tighten to macroscopic form (2026-05-17, 2e9ad7d b453ce4, #352)
Background: rules/plan-principles.md (PR #343) had three gaps: §1 read as symmetric-coverage only (no goal-shortest-path framing); no cross-cutting SSOT principle; Codex adversarial reviewers and reviewer agents (outline-reviewer, detail-reviewer) had no reference to these principles, making the planner/reviewer reference graph asymmetric. Reference sites also restated the principle TOC at every call — violating the very SSOT principle being added.
Changes: Renamed rules/plan-principles.md to rules/core-principles.md. Rewrote §1 to lead with "Solve from the class, not from the immediate task" — root/abstract/parent class identification first, then optional merge/replace/restructure of the class itself, then sibling application. Added §4 Single Source of Truth (reference master / no duplication / extract shared part; excludes summaries/snapshots/caches/stream records). Tightened §1–§4 to macroscopic form: dropped Apply when / How / Checks organizational headers; removed concrete example enumerations; §2 compressed to its principle (orthogonality = §1 specialized to symmetric pairs). Added core-principles reference bullets to agents/outline-reviewer.md and agents/detail-reviewer.md. Extended bin/review-plan-codex to accept multiple --context flags. Wired --context rules/core-principles.md into all Codex call sites: skills/make-outline-plan/SKILL.md, skills/make-detail-plan/SKILL.md. Applied SSOT to references to this file itself: every reference site (CLAUDE.md, planners, reviewers, SKILLs, rules/coding.md) reduced to bare "rules/core-principles.md" — no TOC restatement. Tests: added B8-B13 and test-30 (multi-context).


### BUGFIX: backfill: has_appended_sentinel idempotency broken — jq 'm' flag does not anchor '^' to line starts (Oniguruma semantics) (2026-05-17, #364)
Background: jq's 'm' flag (Oniguruma) is multi-line dot mode, not PCRE line-anchor mode — ^ still anchors to string start. has_appended_sentinel() used test('^...appended'; 'm') which failed to detect merged-format sentinels where the sentinel appears on line 2 of the comment body. Root cause: R32 mock dispatched on 'm' substring presence rather than exercising real jq behavior.
Changes: Replace regex with (^|\n)<!-- issue-close-sentinel: appended (no 'm' flag). Update R32 mock dispatch and R33 grep regression guard in tests/feature-issue-222.sh and tests/fixtures/gh-mock/gh to match new pattern via (^|\n) substring check.


### FEATURE: Split /issue-close into Phase 1 (worktree) and Phase 2 (main) skills (2026-05-17, 3934325, #325)
Background: /issue-close ran from the main worktree to append docs/history.md, forcing the user to toggle ENFORCE_WORKTREE off/on each invocation (with rollback risk if forgotten). Discovered most acutely during #262's close, which compounded with a CRLF/UU rebase artifact (#277 thread). PR #323 attempted a path-based bypass via .env.example default but was closed after Codex review surfaced cross-repo suffix collision and lack of append-only enforcement.
Changes: Split /issue-close into /issue-close-stage (Phase 1, runs INSIDE the linked worktree before PR merge — sub-issue gate, pending sentinel, doc-append + commit, promote sentinel, parent body update) and /issue-close-finalize (Phase 2, runs from main worktree AFTER merge — API-only on the normal path: close issue, post resolved-by + appended sentinels). Both new skills carry user-invocable: false so they only fire from CLAUDE.md Steps 8.5 / 10b. /commit-push pre-flights Phase 1 completion via check-phase1-complete.sh and embeds <!-- issue-close-pr-of: N --> in the PR body; /issue-close-finalize resolves the merge SHA via find-pr-by-marker.sh. Triage logic was extracted into issue-close-triage-lib.sh (SSOT). Legacy /issue-close skill deleted. Follow-up gaps filed as #360 (pending-sentinel rollback), #361 (PR/SHA early-resolve masking errors), #366 (gate-skip when parent already CLOSED).


### REFACTOR: Remove cc-session-title — VS Code auto-titler overwrites every turn (#299) (2026-05-17, #299)
Background: PRs #303/#313/#331 added a workflow-driven session-title chain (set-issue / add-pr / mark-complete) that wrote custom-title JSONL records into ~/.claude/projects/<encoded-cwd>/<session>.jsonl. The VS Code Claude Code extension's auto-titler runs after every Stop hook and unconditionally overwrites custom-title, making the chain a permanent no-op regardless of when (Stop, SessionStart, or any other hook) the workflow rewrites the title. No last-writer-wins variant can win against per-turn overwrite. User decided to abandon the feature.
Changes: Deleted bin/cc-session-title.py and its two test files (test_feat-cc-session-title.py, test_fix-session-title-set-issue-guard.py). Removed cc-session-title invocations from skills/workflow-init, clarify-intent, commit-push, worktree-end. Removed launcher-generation blocks from install/linux/dotfileslink.sh and install/win/dotfileslink.ps1, and added idempotent BEGIN/END temporary cleanup blocks to actively remove ~/.local/bin/cc-session-title{,.cmd} on next dotfileslink run. Dropped A9 regression assertion from tests/feature-workflow-init-survey-shift-left.sh.


### REFACTOR: Add .py to cc-session-title installer cleanup list (#299) (2026-05-17)
Background: Initial removal commit only purged ~/.local/bin/cc-session-title{,.cmd}; the .py file (deployed by an earlier iteration of the launcher) was missed.
Changes: Extended cleanup arrays in install/linux/dotfileslink.sh and install/win/dotfileslink.ps1 to also remove ~/.local/bin/cc-session-title.py on next dotfileslink run.


### FEATURE: Phase 1: gate gh operations by remote host (2026-05-17, #367)
Background: agents workflow assumed GitHub remote implicitly. On non-GitHub hosts (generic git hosting) all gh CLI calls failed. Phase 1 auto-detects non-GitHub remote and skips gh operations to allow the rest of the workflow (plan/test/commit/push) to function.
Changes: Add bin/is-github-dotcom-remote helper (exit 0=GitHub, 1=non-GitHub, 2=unknown). Gate workflow-init, clarify-intent, commit-push, issue-close-stage, issue-close-finalize against non-GitHub remotes. Add tests and manual review checklist.


### BUGFIX: issue-close-finalize: skip sub-issue gate on auto_close_path when parent already CLOSED (#366) (2026-05-18)
Background: auto_close_path route (issues closed via 'closes #N' without Phase 1) included Step B (sub-issue gate) in NEXT_STEPS. The gate's intent is pre-close protection; once the parent is CLOSED it only stalls bookkeeping behind long-lived tracker sub-issues indefinitely.
Changes: Drop B from auto_close_path NEXT_STEPS in issue-close-finalize-triage.sh. Update SKILL.md Step B header to clarify Phase 1 only scope. Add FT5b trap test with closed_no_sentinel_open_subissue fixture.


### BUGFIX: issue-close-finalize: defer PR/SHA resolution to after triage, guarded by J-step membership (#361) (2026-05-18)
Background: find-pr-by-marker.sh was invoked unconditionally in the SKILL.md Pre-flight section before triage ran. Recovery routes (resume_h, stuck_sentinel_only) that do not include Step J would abort with 'PR not found' rather than surfacing the real diagnostic.
Changes: Remove find-pr-by-marker.sh from Pre-flight. Add Step A.5 with [[ ,, == *,J,* ]] guard so PR/SHA resolution only runs when the resolved-by sentinel step is scheduled. Add static contract test feature-361-finalize-pr-resolution-order.sh verifying ordering and guard presence.


### FEATURE: Add WIP signaling via Projects v2 Status field (#362) (2026-05-18, #362)
Background: GitHub Issues has only open/closed states; parallel Claude Code sessions have no way to signal one session is actively working an issue. Projects v2 Status and a session-fingerprint text field (sha256(session_id:N)[:8]) provide the signaling layer.
Changes: New bin/github-issues/wip-state.sh helper with set/check/clear/setup verbs. Integrated into clarify-intent Completion (set after intent:clarified label), workflow-init Step 3 (check on OPEN issue; AskUserQuestion on conflict), and issue-close-finalize Step K (clear on close). Updated issue-close-finalize-triage.sh to include K in all NEXT_STEPS paths. Added tests/feature-wip-state.sh (31 cases), feature-issue-close-finalize-wip-clear.sh (6 cases), and WIP assertions to feature-clarify-intent.sh and feature-workflow-init-routing.sh.


### FEATURE: /issue-create: auto-survey existing issues before creating (2026-05-18, #378)
Background: Every time the user creates a new issue via /issue-create, they must manually instruct survey/reopen/sub-issue/sibling steps. This automates all five outcomes inside the skill using a new dispatch wrapper script.
Changes: New bin/github-issues/issue-create-dispatch.sh dispatch script handles five verdicts (none/reopen/sub-of/make-parent/sibling). skills/issue-create/SKILL.md rewritten to 4-phase procedure: Gather/Survey/Confirm/Dispatch. rules/github-issues.md updated with new survey behavior and sub-issue node-id clarification.


### FEATURE: wip-state.sh setup: auto-create session-fingerprint field if missing (2026-05-19, #383)
Background: cmd_setup hard-errored when the session-fingerprint Projects v2 text field did not exist, requiring manual UI creation. Broke zero-touch onboarding for new repos and bulk issue migration (#247).
Changes: Added ensure_field helper inside cmd_setup: auto-creates missing session-fingerprint field via createProjectV2Field mutation, re-discovers it after creation, idempotent no-op when field exists. Added project-scope hard-gate (gh auth status check) before mutation.


### FEATURE: issue-migration: reusable helper scripts for history/todo migration to other repos (2026-05-19, #247)
Background: The nirecom/agents migration (history.md → closed issues, todo.md → open issues, Projects v2 Content Date backfill) was performed ad hoc with repo-specific scripts. To roll this out to other repos, reusable helpers are needed under bin/github-issues/migration/.
Changes: Added state.sh / orchestrate.sh / create-project.sh under bin/github-issues/migration/. Parameterized the existing migrate-history.sh / migrate-todo.sh / backfill-content-date.sh / preview-history.sh / backfill-commit-comments.sh with a REPO_DIR argument. Made the flow resumable via a canary pattern (1 → confirm → 2 → confirm → all) and a state machine (.migration-state.json). Also fixed a todo.md → ID index rewrite bug in migrate-todo.sh. The /migrate-repo skill (skills/migrate-repo/SKILL.md) launches the whole flow in one shot. The original issue body assumed .py as the implementation language, but .sh was adopted to match the convention of the neighboring scripts.


### FEATURE: worktree-end: deferred-resume cleanup for Windows EPERM (closes #251 and #357) (2026-05-19, #251, #357)
Background: Step 6c (git worktree remove) of /worktree-end fails with EPERM on Windows + VS Code. The previous in-session workarounds (#294: 'cd <main>' chain; #333: Reload Window) could not release the session-level CWD lock held by the VS Code extension host when the session had entered the worktree via the EnterWorktree tool. The variant resurfaced as #357 and could only be recovered by a full Claude Code restart.
Changes: Introduced a deferred-resume escape path. When Step 6c fails with EPERM/busy/not-empty, /worktree-end writes a 3-line marker at ~/.workflow-plans/worktree-end/pending-cwd-unlock-<repo-id>--<branch> (branch / worktree-path / 'pre-remove' stage) and exits gracefully with instructions to run /worktree-end --resume in a new session. The new entry point bin/worktree-end-resume-load.js validates the marker (WORKTREE_BASE_DIR path-traversal guard, KNOWN_STAGES whitelist) and replays the remaining cleanup (worktree remove, prune, orphan-dir, branch -D, fetch + pull). hooks/enforce-worktree.js is generalized to support multiple marker prefixes via a MARKER_PREFIXES constant plus prefix-loop dispatch in isMarkerFilePath / isAllowedMarkerDelete (PR1). 52 tests pass; security review PASS.


### FEATURE: sentinel chain guard + rules/git.md write commands expansion (#382) (2026-05-20)
Background: Sentinel echo commands chained with && could bypass hook classification in workflow-gate.js (the sentinel echo dropped silently while the chained non-sentinel command ran unguarded). Separately, rules/git.md only covered git add/commit/push, leaving other write subcommands (fetch, pull, merge, rebase, worktree, stash, etc.) undocumented as chain-prohibited.
Changes: Extracted shared sentinel recognition primitives to hooks/lib/sentinel-patterns.js (SSOT refactor; workflow-mark.js now imports from it). Added sentinel chain guard to hooks/workflow-gate.js blocking echo <<WORKFLOW_*>> && <non-sentinel> forms. Expanded rules/git.md Write Commands section to enumerate all git write subcommands. Added test suite tests/feature-sentinel-and-chain-gate.sh (15 cases, all passing).


### INCIDENT: #1: Subagent autonomously ran winget install (jq) without user approval (2026-05-20, #384)
Cause: A general-purpose subagent launched by write-tests detected missing jq and autonomously ran winget install --id jqlang.jq --silent without user approval. rules/user-escalation.md Rule 1 (Autonomy-First) had no carve-out for system-state-altering commands, and rules/ops.md did not list package manager commands as requiring approval.
Fix: Short-term: user manually ran winget uninstall --id jqlang.jq. Long-term: added hooks/enforce-system-ops.js PreToolUse hook blocking categories A-F (pkg install, power, service stop/disable/mask, user/group mgmt, reg/boot, disk ops). Updated rules/installer.md, rules/ops.md, and rules/user-escalation.md. Installer scripts bypass via inherited SYSTEM_OPS_APPROVED=1 env var.


### FEATURE: Review the CC↔Codex plan-review loop: rounds overrun the cap and quality plateaus (#329) (2026-05-20, #329)
Background: Sessions using make-detail-plan experienced cap overruns (rounds 3-4) and quality plateau: codex raised 5 new concerns each round with no cumulative improvement, re-raising already-resolved items. Accepted design decisions (tradeoffs) were not being propagated to codex, causing repeated misreads. Extension budget was unbounded in practice.
Changes: Added SSOT functions to bin/lib/codex-core.sh: severity vocab, round-log (jq), hard-cap check, validator, jq prereq guard. Added bin/extract-accepted-tradeoffs (awk-based markdown extractor) and bin/review-loop-cap-menu (JSON-emitting cap decision helper, exit 42 auto-extend). Rewrote bin/review-plan-codex with --cap/--max-extensions/--extensions-used/--session-id/--log-dir/--accepted-tradeoffs args; fail-closed log-before-emit contract. Updated skills/clarify-intent, make-outline-plan, make-detail-plan SKILL.md and agents/detail-planner.md, outline-planner.md, outline-reviewer.md: accepted-tradeoffs carry-forward, round-log, planner-response trailer. Extracted symmetric codex review loop + cap-menu dispatch into skills/_shared/ (SSOT, removes ~150 lines of SKILL.md duplication). Added jq prereq docs to README.md. Added tests/feature-329-*.sh (58 new cases).


### FEATURE: Group: Better way to allow /issue-close's docs/history.md append from main (2026-05-20, 78d0796, #325)
Background: Phase 1 (issue-close-stage) wrote history.md inside the linked worktree and committed it on the feature branch, causing extra history-only commits, rebase conflicts on parallel branches (#277), staged-but-uncommitted leftovers (#280), and a gap where auto_close_path could not write history.md from the main worktree under ENFORCE_WORKTREE=on.
Changes: Moved doc-append + git commit from Phase 1 to Phase 2 (issue-close-finalize). Added isAllowedHistoryWriteViaIssueCloseSkill bypass to enforce-worktree.js (three-axis AND: ISSUE_CLOSE_SKILL=1 prefix + docs/history path + git-add/commit shape). Relaxed check-phase1-complete.sh to sentinel-only. Updated issue-close-stage-triage.sh routing to B,D,F,G. Side-effects: #277 and #280 resolved; auto_close_path gap closed.


### FEATURE: workflow-gate: mode-aware user_verification prompt + resolveRepoDir payload-cwd tier (2026-05-20, #380, #394)
Background: When git commit ran without -C from a linked worktree, resolveRepoDir() fell back to the main worktree CWD, causing isWorktreeContext() to return false. This pushed user_verification into incomplete[], and SKILL_MAP[user_verification]'s unconditional 'run immediately' wording caused Claude to override CLAUDE.md Step 8's 'ENFORCE_WORKTREE=on: No action here' rule and emit the sentinel unnecessarily.
Changes: Added isLinkedWorktree() predicate to workflow-gate.js (narrower than isWorktreeContext — checks only common-dir != git-dir). Extended resolveRepoDir() with a Tier 3 that trusts hook payload input.cwd when it resolves to a linked worktree, preventing CWD drift on Windows VS Code (CC #27343). Updated SKILL_MAP[user_verification] text with explicit two-branch structure using pipe separator (ENFORCE_WORKTREE=on+linked-worktree → SKIP | otherwise → emit). Added C17b (positive) and C17c (negative) regression tests to feature-commit-gate-redesign.sh.


### FEATURE: workflow-gate: resolveRepoDir CWD fallback breaks worktree commit skip when git -C is omitted (2026-05-20, #394, #380)
Background: Sub-issue of #225 pattern 4. When git commit ran without -C from a linked worktree, resolveRepoDir() returned the main worktree CWD (process.cwd() drift on Windows VS Code, CC #27343). isWorktreeContext() then received the main worktree path, returned false, and incorrectly pushed user_verification into the incomplete list — blocking commits that should have been approved.
Changes: Root cause addressed as part of #380 fix: added isLinkedWorktree() predicate and resolveRepoDir() Tier 3 that trusts hook payload input.cwd when it resolves to a linked worktree. See #380 history entry for full changeset.


### FEATURE: refactor-prompts skill + prompt-criteria.md SSOT (#395) (2026-05-20)
Background: rules/*.md (ops.md, git.md, shell-commands.md) were redundantly enumerating the same dangerous command literals that hooks/lib/bash-write-patterns.js and settings.json deny already block. Prompt-quality criteria (directives-not-prose, one-line-per-directive, few-examples) were also scattered across multiple files with no SSOT. Issue #395 introduced the tooling to fix this systematically.
Changes: Added rules/prompt-criteria.md as SSOT for prompt-content quality criteria (Form, Examples discipline, SSOT specialization) — scoped via frontmatter globs to rules/skills/agents. Added /refactor-prompts skill (SKILL.md 25 lines) with 2-phase scan: lexical scan via bin/refactor-prompts/ CLI (extract-keywords.js reads bash-write-patterns.js + settings.permissions.deny + enforce-system-ops.js; scan-prompts.js matches hot regions in rules/*.md / skills/*/SKILL.md / agents/*.md with word-boundary regex and whitespace normalization) + LLM judge (agents/refactor-prompts-judge.md owns the verdict semantics: delete/category-rewrite/keep-trigger/keep-context/defer). Defer regions surface in PR body only. Updated bin/review-skill-size to reference the SSOT. 25 tests (extract 10, scan 9, skill 6 incl. handoff contract). Closes #395.


### FEATURE: fix(migration): create-project.sh uses non-existent `gh -C` flag (2026-05-20, 3040e1d8a5bfc13b00f45009274d88c620bc8d99, #406)
Background: `bin/github-issues/migration/create-project.sh` lines 32-33 use `gh -C "$REPO_DIR" repo view ...`, but the `gh` CLI does not support a `-C` flag (that pattern belongs to `git`). As a result, Step 4 (Projects v2 + Content Date backfill) of `/migrate-repo` always fails with: ``` unknown shorthand flag: 'C' in -C Usage:  gh repo view [<repository>] [flags] ``` Reproduced today while migrating my-private-repo. Steps 1-3 and 5 of `/migrate-repo` work; only Step 4 is broken.
Changes: - Replace `gh -C "$REPO_DIR" repo view ...` with `(cd "$REPO_DIR" && gh repo view ...)` on lines 32-33. - Verify no other scripts under `bin/github-issues/` use the same incorrect pattern. Repro: ``` bash bin/github-issues/migration/orchestrate.sh <fresh-repo> ``` Related to #247 (the parent issue that introduced these scripts).


### FEATURE: #404: Enforce reason on WORKFLOW sentinels; ENFORCE_WORKTREE_ON to allow (2026-05-21, dba1018,5434853)
Background: Issue #404: bare (reason-less) sentinel forms for WORKFLOW_USER_VERIFIED, WORKFLOW_ENFORCE_WORKTREE_OFF, and WORKFLOW_ENFORCE_WORKTREE_ON created audit gaps and gave LLM no self-reflection cue. ENFORCE_WORKTREE_ON (restoring enforcement) required an unnecessary ask dialog despite being low-risk.
Changes: Made reason field mandatory at both layers (settings.json and sentinel-patterns.js regex). Bare forms now hard-rejected via LOOKSLIKE handler in workflow-mark.js. ENFORCE_WORKTREE_ON: * moved from ask to allow. Added repo-wide grep audit gate (feature-bare-sentinel-audit.sh). Fixed pre-existing claude-global path bug in 2 test files. Soft-validation preserved: validateSkipReason warns but applies for these 3 sentinels.


### INCIDENT: #2: #415: canary gate bypass via yes y | pipe during /migrate-repo (2026-05-21, 46a990a4)
Cause: orchestrate.sh chained canary-1 -> confirm() -> canary-2 -> confirm() -> full within a single process. confirm() read from stdin via read -rp, which yes y | bypasses entirely. TTY detection ([ -t 0 ]) is defeated by PTY wrappers, so behavioral guards were ineffective.
Fix: Approach B (structural separation): added --stage canary-1|canary-2|full flag to orchestrate.sh. Each stage runs as one process invocation and exits, making cross-stage bypass structurally impossible. Added schema v2 to state.sh (advanced{} blocks per kind) with state_set_advanced/state_get_advanced helpers. PR #422 (merge 46a990a4).


### FEATURE: Skillize code phase (Step 5 subagent delegation + per-language guidance) (2026-05-21, aa7ecdc, #411)
Background: CLAUDE.md Step 5 (code phase) was inlined; main-context token cost and the lack of language-specific guidance motivated extracting it as a subagent-delegated skill, mirroring /run-tests.
Changes: Added /write-code skill (subagent-delegated edit + lint/typecheck/self-repair, CONFIRM_CODE gate). Moved skills/judge-task-complexity/SKILL.md to skills/_shared/judge-task-complexity.md (read-only rubric); updated path references in make-detail-plan, write-tests, write-code. Added rules/coding/python.md and rules/coding/nodejs.md as B-layer language essence. Added tests/feature-write-code-skill-static.sh.


### FEATURE: find-pr-by-marker.sh: stale marker incorrectly treated as closing PR (2026-05-21, 293b88b, #418)
Background: find-pr-by-marker.sh used the issue-close-pr-of body marker as primary PR source. Stale markers from abandoned branches were trusted, causing wrong merge commit and PR number in resolved-by sentinels.
Changes: Swapped primary/fallback: closedByPullRequestsReferences (most-recently merged) is now primary, marker is fallback. Added validate_sentinel_freshness() to triage-lib.sh (ISSUE_CLOSE_STALE_DAYS env, default 30d); stale pending sentinel is treated as Phase 1 not run.


### FEATURE: Phase 2 history.md write: non-GitHub remote gap and issue body insufficiency (2026-05-21, 293b88b, #412)
Background: Phase 2 issue-to-history.sh assumed gh issue view, breaking on non-GitHub remotes. Issue body alone is also insufficient for history.md because implementation notes (rejected alternatives, sharp edges) are only available inside the worktree during the PR. CLOSED:appended triage path omitted Step E entirely.
Changes: Added History Notes section to WORKTREE_NOTES.md template. issue-to-history.sh now accepts --history-notes-file, --non-github-mode, --title, --body-file, --closed-date. CLOSED:appended triage NEXT_STEPS changed from J,K to E,J,K.


### FEATURE: Long-lived pending sentinel has no rollback or expiry when Phase 1 branch is abandoned (2026-05-21, 293b88b, #360)
Background: Phase 1 posted an issue-close-sentinel comment with no TTL. If the branch was abandoned or rebased, the stale sentinel confused triage on re-attempt, routing through recovery instead of running Phase 1 cleanly.
Changes: Added validate_sentinel_freshness() in issue-close-triage-lib.sh using node-based ISO8601 diff. Controlled by ISSUE_CLOSE_STALE_DAYS env var (default 30d); fail-open on malformed/future dates. Stale pending sentinel is treated as absent, triggering clean Phase 1 re-run.


### FEATURE: fix(migration): backfill-content-date.sh fails on Windows due to CRLF in jq output (2026-05-22, adda42a, #427)
Background: On Windows, jq.exe opens stdout in text mode and emits CRLF-terminated lines. backfill-content-date.sh captured the migrated issue numbers via $(jq -r ...) and fed them through 'while read', leaving \r attached to each number; subsequent 'gh issue view 3\r' then failed with 'invalid issue format'. The same latent CRLF risk existed in every scalar-capture site across the migration scripts. MSYS Git Bash's $(...) strips trailing CRLF, masking the bug on developer laptops.
Changes: Added jq_text() SSOT helper in bin/github-issues/migration/state.sh that wraps 'jq -r | tr -d \r' inside a subshell with pipefail so jq's exit code propagates through the tr stage. Routed all confirmed scalar-capture jq calls in state.sh, backfill-content-date.sh, create-project.sh, and orchestrate.sh through jq_text. Replaced create-project.sh's 'jq ... | head -1' lookup with a SIGPIPE-safe jq array-index pattern (.[0] // empty) to avoid regressing under set -euo pipefail. Added tests/fix-fix-427-crlf-jq-migration.sh covering CRLF input handling and the SIGPIPE pattern.


### FEATURE: forge write scan: extend PreToolUse hook to scan gh issue/pr write commands (2026-05-22)
Background: hooks/scan-outbound.js Bash branch fast-pathed all non-git-commit commands to approve, so gh issue create/edit/comment and gh pr create/edit/comment/review bypassed private-info scanning entirely. Private info in --body, --title, --body-file, and heredoc arguments could be posted to GitHub Issues and PRs without triggering the blocklist.
Changes: Added hooks/lib/forge-write-extract.js (isForgeScanTarget + extractTexts) to classify forge write commands and extract scannable text from --body/--title (quoted, equals-form, unquoted token), --body-file (with Windows path normalization and 1 MiB size cap), and heredoc (arbitrary delimiter). Extended hooks/scan-outbound.js Bash branch to run extractTexts before the git-commit check; forge targets with extracted content flow to bin/scan-outbound.sh --stdin; forge commands with no extractable body (e.g. close-only subcommands) are approved. Updated docs/scan-outbound.md with forge write checkpoint row and v1 known-false-negatives (gh repo *, gh api). Tests: feature-forge-write-scan-extract.sh (27 unit cases), feature-forge-write-scan-pretooluse.sh (15 integration cases including Windows .cmd stub, private-repo skip, --body-file scan, non-EOF heredoc).


### FEATURE: settings.json SSH-path deny pattern false-positive on command body text (2026-05-22, 58cb407daccf7b393261363e1e525f33bd8bb9c7, #424)
Background: During a 2026-05-21 session, running `gh issue create --repo nirecom/my-private-repo` from the agents repo (cwd) returned `Permission to use Bash ... has been denied.` without showing a permission dialog to the user. Investigation found that the following deny pattern at `settings.json` line 204 in this repo was causing a false positive: ``` "Bash(*~/.ssh/*)" ``` Because this pattern is a substring glob, matching is evaluated against the entire command string (including the `--body` argument and heredoc contents). Simply including a string like `~/.ssh/config` in an issue body **for explanatory purposes** triggers an unintended auto-deny. ## Symptoms / Evidence Timestamps from lines 405-413 of session log `0f58b662-ee5d-4bbf-bd31-6ea0699ae16b.jsonl`: | Time (UTC) | Event | |---|---| | 14:16:06.631 | assistant tool_use issued (`gh issue create --repo nirecom/my-private-repo --body "$(cat <<'EOF'...`) | | 14:16:06.797 | hook_success: `scan-outbound.js` | | 14:16:06.821 | hook_success: `block-dotenv.js` | | 14:16:06.822 | hook_success: `enforce-worktree.js` | | 14:16:06.822 | hook_success: `enforce-issue-close.js` | | 14:16:06.824 | hook_success: `enforce-system-ops.js` | | 14:16:06.832 | tool_result: `has been denied` | The deny occurred ~8ms after the hooks passed. **This is not a plausible timescale for a user clicking Deny in a dialog**, confirming it was an auto-deny from Claude Code's built-in `permissions.deny` rule matching. ## Root cause The intended protection target is **direct reads of the SSH private key**, such as `cat ~/.ssh/id_rsa`, but because the implementation does substring matching against the command string, it matches whenever the literal `~/.ssh/` appears anywhere in the command body, regardless of context. It cannot distinguish an actual file access from a mere textual mention. The following two existing rules match against the tool-input file path, so they work correctly and are unaffected (out of scope for this issue): ``` "Read(~/.ssh/**)" "Edit(~/.ssh/**)" ``` ## Proposed fixes ### Option A (recommended): replace with patterns scoped to read commands Keep substring matching, but scope the target to the start of file-read/write style commands. Before: ``` "Bash(*~/.ssh/*)" ``` After (example): ``` "Bash(cat *~/.ssh/*)" "Bash(less *~/.ssh/*)" "Bash(more *~/.ssh/*)" "Bash(head *~/.ssh/*)" "Bash(tail *~/.ssh/*)" "Bash(cp *~/.ssh/*)" "Bash(mv *~/.ssh/*)" "Bash(scp *~/.ssh/*)" "Bash(Get-Content *~/.ssh/*)" "Bash(Copy-Item *~/.ssh/*)" ``` - Pros: self-contained within Claude Code's existing built-in deny, no hook addition needed - Cons: the number of patterns grows. Redirect (`* < ~/.ssh/*`) and pipe-based access need separate patterns ### Option B: perform context-aware detection at the hook layer Introduce a new `block-ssh-private-key.js` PreToolUse hook adopting the same quote-aware tokenizer approach as `block-dotenv.js`, decomposing the command into actual tokens and then judging "whether this is a real file access." Remove the `Bash(*~/.ssh/*)` deny rule from `settings.json`. - Pros: accurate judgment in one place. Can structurally exclude false positives such as mentions inside `gh pr create --body` - Cons: requires adding a hook together with removing the existing deny rule. Effort is small since `block-dotenv.js`'s implementation pattern can be reused as-is ### Evaluation axes | Aspect | Option A | Option B | |---|---|---| | Implementation size | Small (settings.json edit only) | Medium (new hook + removal of existing deny) | | Robustness against gaps | Risk of missing pattern enumeration | Structurally comprehensive via tokenizer | | Consistency with existing patterns | Same approach as surrounding Bash deny rules | Same approach as `block-dotenv.js` | | Maintenance | Must add a pattern each time a new read command appears | Handled via tokenizer improvements | Since `block-dotenv.js` is a precedent, the same approach (Option B) is more consistent for this case too. ## Out of scope - Preserve the intent of protecting SSH private keys (make it precise rather than looser) - The `Read(~/.ssh/**)` / `Edit(~/.ssh/**)` deny rules are already precise and need no change - A comprehensive survey of similar false positives from other substring patterns (e.g. `Bash(*aws * delete*)`) is a separate issue (this issue only fixes the representative example found here) ## Notes - Discovery session: `20260521-221915` (a side effect of attempting to create an issue in my-private-repo for an unrelated matter during the clarify-intent stage) - The my-private-repo issue in question was ultimately created via manual user execution: nirecom/my-private-repo#42 - The reason similar denies were not encountered in past sessions is presumed to be the coincidence of not having included the `~/.ssh/` literal in issue bodies
Changes: Removed the substring-glob deny rule Bash(*~/.ssh/*) from settings.json. Added hooks/block-ssh-private-key.js (PreToolUse), backed by hooks/lib/command-parser.js extracted from block-dotenv.js — tokenize argv, walk path-bearing positions, ignore text-flag values and quoted message bodies. Coverage gains over the old substring rule: runInTerminal/runCommands/editFiles tools, attached redirect forms (>~/.ssh/, <~/.ssh/, 2>~/.ssh/), --flag=value form for path-bearing flags, path normalization (~/./.ssh/, $USERPROFILE/.ssh/, /root/.ssh/), and defense-in-depth wrapper for Read/Grep/Glob/Edit/Write/MultiEdit. block-dotenv.js refactored to share the same engine (core-principles §2 orthogonality). Tests: tests/feature-424-command-parser.sh, tests/fix-issue-424-ssh-deny-hook.sh, tests/lib/test-command-parser.js, tests/main-block-dotenv.sh additions. Related to #253


### FEATURE: worktree-end Step 5.5 should prompt /issue-create for WORKTREE_NOTES.md entries before backup/delete (2026-05-22, 4ee1134e3b95b7aae4a91e1cf2a18d5d64e20744, #429)
Background: `/worktree-end` Step 5.5 currently does: 1. Last-chance findings review (instruction to append more to `WORKTREE_NOTES.md`) 2. Capture PR metadata 3. Copy `WORKTREE_NOTES.md` to backup destination 4. Cache branch/path/date for Final Report The Final Report includes the backup path of `WORKTREE_NOTES.md`, but **no step prompts the user to convert pending BugsFound / RelatedTasks / NextTasks entries into GitHub issues before the worktree is removed**. As a result, entries get archived under `.worktree-backup/<branch>/WORKTREE_NOTES.md` and are effectively forgotten — they never surface back into the active GitHub Issues task list (the SSOT per `rules/github-issues.md`). ## Observed in Session for #423 (commit a5c3c5e). After bundling the `review-code-codex` fallback fix into #423, `WORKTREE_NOTES.md` still had 2 unresolved BugsFound entries and 2 RelatedTasks entries. None would have been issue-ized had the user not explicitly noticed mid-skill. ## Proposed direction In `skills/worktree-end/SKILL.md` Step 5.5, between sub-step (a) and (c): - (a.5) Parse `WORKTREE_NOTES.md`. For each non-empty entry under `## BugsFound`, `## RelatedTasks`, `## NextTasks` (excluding entries already marked resolved/closed):   - Present a brief list to the user   - Ask: which ones to `/issue-create` now? (multi-select)   - For selected entries, invoke `/issue-create` with a body draft derived from the bullet text   - Mark the converted entries in-place (e.g. append `→ #<N>`) so the backup copy reflects the linkage ## Scope notes - Out of scope: auto-deciding which entries are issue-worthy (user judgment required) - Out of scope: closing the loop on entries already converted in prior sessions (idempotent re-parse should detect existing `→ #<N>` markers and skip)
Changes:  (History Notes: Added sub-step (a.5) to worktree-end Step 5.5: parse WORKTREE_NOTES.md BugsFound/RelatedTasks/NextTasks entries, present unpromoted entries via AskUserQuestion multi-select, invoke /issue-create per selected entry, annotate with <!-- promoted: #N --> before backup. Extracted shared lib to hooks/lib/worktree-notes-sections.js, added bin/worktree-notes-triage.js CLI, compacted skills/issue-create/SKILL.md with stdout URL contract.)


### FEATURE: review-code-codex: diff coverage gaps in BASE...HEAD fallback (untracked files; non-empty BASE...HEAD masks fresh edits) (2026-05-22, 1f1d3ac329c91d33c4cf27e5cd1baf2df7267262, #430)
Background: `bin/review-code-codex`'s fallback to `git diff HEAD` (introduced by PR #426) has two diff coverage gaps. Gap 1: when `BASE...HEAD` is empty, untracked files are silently skipped because `git diff HEAD` does not include them, so brand-new files created by `/write-code` get no review. Gap 2: when `BASE...HEAD` is non-empty, the one-way gate causes uncommitted working-tree edits (staged + unstaged) to be entirely ignored, so fresh fixup edits during a second implementation pass are silently missed.
Changes: Extended the diff collection block in `bin/review-code-codex` inline (Approach A from the outline). Added per-file untracked enumeration via `git ls-files --others --exclude-standard -z` + `git diff --no-index /dev/null <file>` pseudo-diff synthesis for Gap 1. Switched committed/uncommitted from a one-way gate to concatenation with `[COMMITTED DIFF (BASE...HEAD)]` / `[UNCOMMITTED CHANGES (working tree vs HEAD)]` separators for Gap 2. Truncation via `MAX_DIFF_LINES=5000` now applies to the combined payload. SKIPPED guard message updated to `"no committed changes vs <base>, no uncommitted changes, no untracked files"`. Added tests 16 (Gap 1 untracked-only) and 17 (Gap 2 committed + uncommitted + untracked combined) to `tests/feature-review-code-codex.sh`. ## Context PR #426 (commit a5c3c5e, bundled into #423) replaced the `git diff --cached` fallback in `bin/review-code-codex` with `git diff HEAD` so workflow Step 6 reviews unstaged working-tree changes (the common state after `/write-code`). This addressed #391's chicken-and-egg case. Codex review of that change surfaced **two further coverage gaps** that the new fallback does not close: ## Gap 1 — untracked files `git diff HEAD` does **not** include untracked files. If `/write-code` creates a brand-new file and leaves it unstaged, `review-code-codex` runs but its prompt has no awareness of that file's content — the review silently skips what is arguably the highest-risk class of change (new code with no prior baseline). ### Repro 1. Fresh feature branch, no commits past base. 2. Create `new-module.ts` (unstaged, untracked). 3. Run `review-code-codex --base main`. 4. Observe: fallback fires ("reviewing uncommitted changes…") but `git diff HEAD` output is empty → SKIPPED with no review. ### Proposed direction In the fallback branch only (when BASE...HEAD is empty): - Enumerate untracked files via `git ls-files --others --exclude-standard -z` - For each, synthesize a pseudo-diff (e.g. `git diff --no-index /dev/null <file>`) - Append to the diff payload sent to codex - Honor the existing `MAX_DIFF_LINES` truncation ## Gap 2 — non-empty BASE...HEAD masks fresh uncommitted edits The fallback is **one-way**: when `git diff BASE...HEAD` is non-empty, uncommitted working-tree changes (staged + unstaged) are entirely ignored. The original rationale ("avoid duplicate/out-of-order content") is sound, but it creates a blind spot: ### Failure mode - Branch has prior commits past `main` AND fresh uncommitted edits (e.g. mid-implementation fixup in a second pass). - `review-code-codex` reviews only the **old committed** content — silently misses the fresh edits the user is actually about to commit. ### Proposed direction Concatenate (committed diff) + (uncommitted diff) with a clear separator like: ``` [COMMITTED DIFF (BASE...HEAD)] ... [UNCOMMITTED CHANGES (working tree vs HEAD)] ... ``` Then truncate the combined payload to `MAX_DIFF_LINES`. The duplicate-content concern from the original comment is overstated: `BASE...HEAD` and `HEAD` (working tree) are disjoint by definition. ## Out of scope - Workflow-gate ordering changes (covered by #225) - Test gaps (the existing test for the bundled fix asserts PERFORMED but doesn't validate the actual prompt content includes the diff — file separately if pursued)


### FEATURE: wip-state.sh: add CLAUDE_SESSION_ID fallback and suppress spurious clear warning (2026-05-22, c3bd6ec, #440)
Background: VS Code Claude Code does not propagate CLAUDE_ENV_FILE to Bash subprocesses, so resolve_session_id() in wip-state.sh always returned rc=2 in that environment. WIP signaling (session fingerprint writes and parallel-session collision detection) was silently broken for all VS Code sessions.
Changes: Added CLAUDE_SESSION_ID direct-env fallback to resolve_session_id() (file-first, env-fallback order). Suppressed spurious fingerprint-clear warning when gh returns rc=1 + 'no changes to make' on an already-empty field. Added T-new-6/7/8 to feature-wip-state.sh covering the new resolution paths.


### FEATURE: issue-close-finalize: fail-soft Step E so J/K always run (2026-05-22, c3bd6ec, #439)
Background: When Step E (doc-append + commit) failed due to a gh/network error, issue-close-finalize would abort before Step H (issue close), J (resolved-by sentinel), and K (WIP clear), leaving the issue in an inconsistent open state.
Changes: Step E failure now emits a warning and proceeds to Step G/H/J/K. End report includes Step E outcome line with /issue-reconcile backfill hint. Steps H/J/K remain mandatory regardless of Step E result.

### FEATURE: issue-create: enforce canonical Background/Changes (or Cause/Fix) schema at issue creation time (2026-05-22, b035f98a7717d6504d14d86c1760da7512413b7b, #443)
Background: `/issue-close-finalize` Step E calls `doc-append` via `bin/github-issues/issue-to-history.sh`, and `bin/github-issues/lib/extract-field.sh` extracts only the 4 canonical fields (`Background`, `Changes`, `Cause`, `Fix`) from the issue body. FEATURE/REFACTOR/BUGFIX types require `Background` + `Changes`; INCIDENT types require `Cause` + `Fix`. If these are empty, `doc-append` errors out and blocks issue-close Step E. However, `/issue-create` does not validate or enforce this canonical schema at issue creation time. As a result, schema mismatches go unnoticed until right before close, and a retroactive `gh issue edit` becomes necessary during close. This is the symmetric counterpart to #401 (graceful fallback on the receiving side, in `issue-to-history.sh`) — a preventive measure on the sending side is worth considering independently, and adopting both would give double protection.
Changes: - Parse the body at `/issue-create` time and check for the presence of the canonical fields - Switch the required field set based on the type label (`type:task` / `type:incident`):   - `type:task` (FEATURE/REFACTOR/BUGFIX): `## Background` and `## Changes` required   - `type:incident`: `## Cause` and `## Fix` required - When absent, decide on a policy among: (a) hard fail and abort creation / (b) warn + auto-insert a fill-in template / (c) confirm via AskUserQuestion - Validate with the same regex as `extract-field.sh` (H2/H3/inline, case-insensitive) to preserve SSOT ## Repro 1. Existing issue #430 was created with only `## Context` / `## Gap 1` / `## Gap 2` / `## Out of scope` 2. After the PR merged, `/issue-close-finalize --from-session` stopped at Step E with `Error: requires --background and --changes` 3. Recovered by retroactively adding `## Background` + `## Changes` via `gh issue edit 430 --body-file` ## Out of scope - Changing `extract-field.sh`'s behavior — considered separately under #401 - Close-path fallback (e.g. using `# H1 title` when canonical fields are absent) — the root cause is the lack of validation at creation time, so fix it there instead - Retroactive consistency check against `history.md` (quality audit of existing entries) — candidate for a separate issue Related to #401


### FEATURE: reduce clicks: CONFIRM_*=off suppresses VS Code file open; Proceed closes plan tab (2026-05-22, 10bac0b58bb1de31707ad0e1de89ae61dff5d250, #445)
Background: Two click-reduction improvements are needed in the workflow's planning pipeline (the intent/outline/detail.md confirmation flow). ## Problem 1: automatically close the plan file in VS Code after Proceed - After selecting **Proceed** in the 'OK to proceed?' dialog, the plan file (intent/outline/detail.md) opened in VS Code must currently be closed manually. - It should not close when Revise is selected (so the file remains available for reference while making corrections). - Since the VS Code CLI has no command to close a file, the means of achieving this needs to be worked out at the outline-plan stage. ## Problem 2: do not open the file in VS Code when `CONFIRM_*=off` - Even with `CONFIRM_DETAIL=off` set, detail.md still gets opened in VS Code (likewise for CONFIRM_OUTLINE / CONFIRM_INTENT). - Target for fix: `hooks/show-plan-link.js` should read the corresponding `CONFIRM_*` flag and skip `openInVsCode()` when it is off. - The systemMessage breadcrumb in chat ('Plan file written: ...') may continue to be shown. ## Constraints - VS Code CLI constraint: the means of achieving Problem 1 requires investigation. - Steps 1+2 of `skills/_shared/confirm-plan.md` are currently mandatory regardless of `CONFIRM_*`, but for Problem 2 only the VS Code open portion of Step 2 should be made conditional. - Same class of problem as #425 (open): needs a conflict check. <!-- auto-created by clarify-intent -->
Changes: Two click-reduction improvements are needed in the workflow's planning pipeline (the intent/outline/detail.md confirmation flow).

### FEATURE: backfill-commit-comments.sh: refine Tier 1.5 to avoid bulk-import-commit collision (sub-issue of #222) (2026-05-22, 32c404995e53609f50c433111fd3ca19ee3aae18, #344)
Background: Introducing Tier 1.5 (`git log --all --reverse -S "<title>" -- docs/history.md docs/history/`) in #316 brought M1 dry-run hash-discovery coverage from 3.3% to 100% (161/188 backfill targets + 27 skipped, zero no-hash). However, close inspection of the dry-run results reveals a **collision where the same hash is attributed to many issues**: | Hash | Count | Identity | |---|---|---| | `3969773` | 12-39 (fluctuates on re-measurement) | `feat(agents-split): add 39 tests and docs from dotfiles (step 11)` — the commit that bulk-imported history.md from dotfiles | | `feedbac` | 5 | (needs investigation) | | 2 other pairs | a few sets | (needs investigation) | Cause: Tier 1.5 returns the oldest commit via `git log --all --reverse -S "<needle>"`. Because a bulk import commit adds 60+ history entries at once, all of these issues resolve to the same import commit. Since this is not the **actual fix commit** for each issue, posting `Resolved by commit 3969773` results in incorrect attribution. ## Scope Improve Tier 1.5 of `bin/github-issues/backfill-commit-comments.sh` with one or a combination of the following: ### Option A: import commit blacklist - Hardcode-exclude known bulk import commits (`3969773` and others) - On a match, fall back to the next-oldest commit, or the next tier if none - **Pro**: minimal implementation, high immediate effect - **Con**: future bulk import commits must be added each time ### Option B: needle refinement - Fallback for when the title, after being stripped by the current `sed -E 's/^[A-Z]+: //'`, is too short/generic - Increase uniqueness by using the whole entry (heading + first line of Background) as the needle - **Pro**: higher precision - **Con**: may miss if the heading itself changed since import time ### Option C: detect "when the same hash is attributed to multiple issues" and demote to no-hash - Aggregate hash → issue list during the dry-run pass - If a threshold (e.g. 3 or more sharing the same hash) is exceeded, distrust that hash - **Pro**: automatic detection, no blacklist needed - **Con**: becomes a 2-pass structure, increasing implementation complexity ### Option D: heuristic "if N entries are added in the same commit, treat it as an import" - Measure the number of entries added via `git show --stat <commit> -- docs/history.md docs/history/` - 5 or more = classified as an import commit and excluded - **Pro**: automatic, general-purpose - **Con**: possible misclassification against things like rotation commits ## Immediate hotfix (already applied in this session via separate PR) As a stopgap until the detection logic is improved, a small fix hardcode-blacklisting `3969773` is planned to land first via a separate PR (separate from this issue, for immediate M2/M3 progress). This issue addresses the more fundamental improvement. ## Acceptance Criteria - Implement at least one of the options - Hash collisions are substantially reduced in the M1 dry-run (no more hashes shared by 3+ entries, or only explicitly legitimate sharing remains) - Existing R-series tests stay green, new collision-detection tests added ## Out of Scope - M2 canary / M3 full run (assumed already done by the time the hotfix lands) - Adding the import-commit hardcode (handled by the hotfix) ## References - Parent issue: #222 (GitHub migration) - Preceding: #316 (introduced Tier 0a/0b/1.5) — closed at bec10e1 - Implementation file: `bin/github-issues/backfill-commit-comments.sh` - Observed collision samples: `3969773` (12-39), `feedbac` (5)
Changes: However, close inspection of the dry-run results reveals a **collision where the same hash is attributed to many issues**:


### FEATURE: workflow: mandate 'class enumeration' at the clarify-intent / outline stages (2026-05-23, da5e41f5ee19b35b24dc6aca8067e41b920ed598, #462)
Background: The survey stage of `/clarify-intent` and `/make-outline-plan` is currently 'symptom-based,' focusing on the single instance being fixed. However, because the procedure does not include enumerating the **siblings of the whole class the symptom belongs to**, the anti-pattern in core-principles.md §1 'Elevate Perspective' (fixing case A while leaving symmetric B/C/D unaddressed) is repeatedly stepped into. ### Recent concrete example (PR #452 / #371) While trying to resolve 'gh issue create gets blocked from the main worktree' (#369), a chain of siblings making up the class = **the false-positive group of classify()** came to light one after another: - `gh issue create --body "##..."` (heredoc/quoted body) → fixed in #371 (in scope) - `bash <wrapper>.sh ...` (wrapper command invoked via a skill) → remains in #460 (slipped through the Group A override net) - `grep -nE "...>..." file` (generic command with quoted regex) → remains in #460 - `$(... 2>/dev/null)` (command substitution closing paren) → existing #359 — recognized as a sibling but not brought into scope - `<<WORKFLOW_ENFORCE_WORKTREE_OFF>>` sentinel marker → remains in #461 (degradation of the sanctioned escape hatch itself) - multi-line `gh issue create \` (line-continuation form) → an **additional, not-yet-filed sibling** discovered while filing this issue: the possibility that the Group A override doesn't work with multi-line commands Observation (1) from #369: if all siblings of the class had been enumerated at fix time, scope decisions (fix in scope / track separately / wontfix) could have been agreed with the user up front, preventing the after-the-fact discovery and drip-by-drip surfacing that occurred after the PR merged. ### Root cause - survey-code looks at 'code structure' but does not cover the 'class of symptoms' - survey-history looks at git/issue history but does not organize it as 'members of a class' - clarify-intent has no built-in question for class identification + sibling enumeration - the outline-planner subagent prompt also has no requirement to enumerate siblings - intent.md / outline.md's Output Schema has no 'Class members' field
Changes: - Add a "Class identification" question to `skills/clarify-intent/SKILL.md`:   - "What class is this bug/task an instance of?"   - "Where else could other members of that class exist? (grep dispatch sites in the implementation + the input space)"   - "Do existing issues/PRs describe a similar class? (keyword search on the class name)" - Add a "list class siblings" requirement to the outline-planner subagent prompt in `skills/make-outline-plan/SKILL.md` - Make a `## Class members` section mandatory in intent.md's Output Schema. Record `fix in scope / track separately / wontfix` for each member, agreed with the user - Add an equivalent handoff field to outline.md's Output Schema too (alongside Considered alternatives) - Affected scope:   - `skills/clarify-intent/SKILL.md`   - `skills/make-outline-plan/SKILL.md`   - `agents/outline-planner.md`   - `tests/feature-clarify-intent-*.sh`, `tests/feature-make-outline-plan-*.sh`   - `rules/core-principles.md` (describe proceduralizing §1 in coordination with the skills) ## Non-goals - Retroactively auditing all existing skills/CLIs (this issue only sets up the procedure for the intent/outline stage) - Duplicate checking at the detail-planner stage (out of scope for this issue; file separately if needed) Related to: - #457 (sibling: the universality rule at implementation time — this issue complements it with class enumeration at planning time) - #369 (parent incident — the concrete case where only observation (1) was used to set scope) - #371 (Group A fix — fixed only 1 member of the class) - #460 (other members of the class remain) - #461 (a class gap in the sanctioned escape hatch)


### FEATURE: install: dotfileslink misses review-loop-cap-menu and review-skill-size launchers (2026-05-23, e9b156b8e91fe3bad7a0b4a07df0718d4159cf3c, #471)
Background: During `/make-detail-plan` cap-menu dispatch, `review-loop-cap-menu` failed with `command not found` (exit 127). The binary exists at `bin/review-loop-cap-menu` in the agents repo but is not on PATH. Root cause: neither `install/win/dotfileslink.ps1` nor `install/linux/dotfileslink.sh` generates a launcher for `review-loop-cap-menu` or `review-skill-size`. They only handle `doc-append`, `doc-append-plain`, `review-code-codex`, `review-plan-codex`. Callers invoke them bare (not via `$AGENTS_CONFIG_DIR/bin/...`): - `skills/_shared/cap-menu-dispatch.md:30` — `menu_json=$(review-loop-cap-menu ...)` - `CLAUDE.md:34` (Step 6) — `review-skill-size --base <merge-base>` So any user without manual PATH augmentation hits exit 127 in the cap-menu dispatch / size-check workflow steps. Related to #244 (same install-launcher gap category; doc-append-plain POSIX side).
Changes: - Add launcher generation for `review-loop-cap-menu` and `review-skill-size` in both `install/win/dotfileslink.ps1` and `install/linux/dotfileslink.sh`, mirroring the `review-code-codex` / `review-plan-codex` pattern (cmd + bash shim on Windows; symlink on Linux). - Audit other `bin/` entries against bare-name invocations under `skills/` and `CLAUDE.md` so every bare-called bin has an installer launcher (orthogonality — close the class, not just the case). ## Repro 1. Fresh checkout of agents repo, run `install/win/dotfileslink.ps1`. 2. New shell: `which review-loop-cap-menu` → not found. 3. Run a `/make-*-plan` flow that reaches cap-menu dispatch → exit 127. Related to #244
